### Window, toolbar and page

In a normal window on the Mac, most of this area draws what the specs describe: the card, its gutter, the centred address field, the page-coloured row, the rail's geometry, the private coat for incognito, copy link and the three screenshots. The problems are in who owns what and how long it lives. The worst is a reachable crash. "Switch to horizontal tabs" is still in the app menu and in the Mac View menu, and choosing it frees the tab strip while two Stedding objects still point at it (WIN-F1). A second crash is a race in the visible-area screenshot, which keeps raw pointers across an asynchronous copy (WIN-F4). The rail's form (labels or glyphs, stacked or flat switcher) follows the collapse state at the start or end of the animation, not the width actually drawn. So every collapse, expand and hover-expand draws the wrong form for a moment, which is the most likely cause of "the rail looks weird" (WIN-F14). The page colour reaches the toolbar through the window-wide colour key. Every colour change re-themes the whole window, a colour cut to 16 levels makes the focused address field grey on white pages, and the previous tab's colour lingers on a tab that is still loading (WIN-F5, F12, F23, F24). The address row and the page colour are owned by SpaceModel, so private windows silently lose them (WIN-F9, F18). The empty part of the address row cannot drag the window (WIN-F2). Nothing in this area explains tabs that do not select on click: every reviewer traced this area's event paths (hit tests, pre-target handlers, the swipe controller, the resize edge) and found none that eats a press. That symptom belongs to the tabs and Spaces areas. The tests here cover helpers and single mixers only, so every finding below passes CI. Nothing was run during the audit: "confirmed" means traced in the code, not reproduced.

#### Features

| Feature | Real state | Why (finding ids) | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Horizontal tab mode (Chromium's "Switch to horizontal tabs") | Broken. Use-after-free on the next tab insert or Space switch. The toolbar disappears if the sidebar was collapsed. Downloads show nowhere. | WIN-F1, F19, F6 | **Cut.** Remove the command from More tools and the Mac View menu, disable IDC_TOGGLE_VERTICAL_TABS, force vertical tabs on under kArcStyleWindow, and state in sidebar.md that Stedding has no horizontal mode. |
| Collapsed rail and hover-expand (sidebar Y6–Y14) | Partly. The geometry is right at rest. The form is wrong during every collapse, expand and hover animation. One click handler leaks per hover-expand. Rail buttons have no accessible name. | WIN-F14, F15, L33–L36 | **Fix.** Decide the form from the drawn width. |
| Sidebar expand/collapse (⌘S), resize edge, width | Works (upstream machinery). No test. | WIN-F17 | **Finish.** Add the view-level test. |
| Address row hides with the sidebar, ⇧⌘D, localhost (toolbar T8, T10–T12) | Partly. Works in normal windows. Missing in private and guest windows, where ⇧⌘D is enabled but does nothing. Also hides in immersive full screen, contrary to T8. A const getter changes state. Two extra synchronous layouts on every tab switch. | WIN-F9, F10, F18, F19, F20, F29, F32, F37 | **Fix.** Give it a per-window owner, make it a passive rule, relayout only when the answer changes. |
| Page-coloured row and contrast icons (toolbar T3, T4, T7, T15, T17, T20, T21) | Partly. The painted row is exact. The focused field, dropdown and separator use the truncated colour (#F0F0F0 on white). Every colour change re-themes the whole window. The provider cache only grows. The old tab's colour stays on a loading tab. | WIN-F5, F12, F23, F24, F30 | **Simplify.** Paint the exact colour directly. Keep only two process-wide contrast suppliers, and re-theme only when the contrast side flips. |
| Address row in macOS full screen (toolbar T6, T24) | The crash guard works. The revealed row may have no painted surface while its icons are coloured for a page bar that is not there (plausible). | WIN-F28, F38, L20–L22 | **Fix** once the dynamic check confirms it (BACKLOG S-63). |
| Centred address field (toolbar T18) | Works, but the centring spacers make the empty row client area: no window drag and no double-click zoom from it. | WIN-F2 | **Fix.** |
| Card, status pill, find bar (toolbar T1, T2, T16; page U1–U4) | Works in normal windows. The pill slides out of the card. The find bar covers the page scrollbar. Popups, PWAs and DevTools get the card treatment too. | L14–L16, L23, L37, L38 | **Fix.** |
| Private window coat (private V1–V3) | Partly. Incognito is right. A normal dark window with Chrome's Grey colour is painted graphite, so it looks private while keeping history. Guest is labelled "Private" without the coat. No page bar in private windows. | WIN-F8, F9, L6, L19 | **Fix.** Use one explicit test (IsIncognitoProfile) and one supplier. |
| Space tint | Works, but a Space tint silently turns off an installed theme, and the comment says the opposite. | L7 | **Fix.** Carry the tint in a Stedding supplier, or document that Spaces replace themes. |
| Sidebar density and text size (sidebar Y1, Y2) | Works, but it is installed from the SpaceModel constructor, so a session that starts with only a private window ignores the preference. | WIN-F9 | **Fix** (installed at startup, part of WIN-6). |
| Downloads on the sidebar button (toolbar T14) | Partly. The ring and badge are drawn. The icon, active state and tooltip are a snapshot taken at construction. The ring colours follow the page bar, not the sidebar. | WIN-F6, F7, L13 | **Fix.** Bind the button to the action item. |
| Row numbers while ⌘ is held (tabs R11–R13) | Partly. The numbers stick after ⌘-Tab to another app. | WIN-F13, L31 | **Fix.** |
| Space switch slide and swipe (spaces B32) | Partly. The slide runs on every switch path on the Mac, but it is a 36 DIP fade of the unpinned list after the swap, and nothing follows the fingers. The swipe never fires on Windows or Linux. | WIN-F16, F31 | **Fix** the Windows swipe. The owner decides between gesture tracking and writing "no tracking" into B32. |
| Bookmarks converted to pins (import I14) | Partly. The bookmark bar and star are off for good. Show Bookmarks Bar flips its check mark and does nothing. | WIN-F11, L1 | **Simplify.** Make the conversion a one-time default instead of a permanent override. |
| Copy link, plain and Markdown (copy-link L1–L6) | Partly. Split view copies both panes. A shift-click range copies hidden tabs. Untitled pages put the tracking query back into the label. Bracketed titles produce broken Markdown. The tracker table is out of date. | WIN-F3, F21, F22, F34, F36, L47–L51 | **Fix.** |
| Screenshots (screenshot C1–C5) | Partly. Use-after-free race on tab close. Debug builds hit a DCHECK on ⇧⌘1. Full page is decoded on the UI thread with no cap. Failures are silent. File names can collide. The peek is ignored. "Show in Finder" reveals a global last path. | WIN-F4, F25, F26, F33, F35, L56–L62 | **Fix.** Restructure around one owned capture job per tab. |
| Short menus (menus M2, M3, M7) | Partly. The page and app tables disagree. The Mac menu bar is not filtered. Translate and the Google help link remain. | L52–L55 | **Fix.** Use one list, applied to all three menus. |
| About version line (settings T10) | Works, but VERSION still says beta.8 on the 155 pin. | L39, L63 | **Fix.** |
| Capture and test hooks in the SteddingArcStyleWindow feature params | Ships in the product. A launcher flag can re-navigate, pin or folder the user's tabs at startup. | L11, L12 | **Cut** from official builds (move to a switch honoured only in non-official builds). |
| Motion gate (motion O1–O3) | Works. | none | Keep. |
| Windows caption buttons over the row (toolbar T19, T22) | Unknown: Windows-only code that cannot be checked on the Mac. It depends on a const getter with side effects. | L17, L18 | **Fix** the getter (WIN-7), then verify on Windows. |

#### Work, in order

- [ ] **WIN-1** (P0) Remove horizontal tab mode under kArcStyleWindow, and tear down the Stedding objects built on the tab strip view before it is cleared: in VerticalTabStripRegionView::OnTabStripViewWillClear, reset space_tab_visibility_ and folder_drag_target_. Disable IDC_TOGGLE_VERTICAL_TABS in BrowserCommandController, drop it from ToolsMenuModel and main_menu_builder.mm, force kVerticalTabsEnabled on, and make AddressRow::SidebarCollapsed also require ShouldDisplayVerticalTabs(). Fixes: WIN-F1, WIN-F19. Done when: in the running browser, neither More tools nor the Mac View menu offers "Switch to horizontal tabs", and a profile whose vertical-tabs pref was off opens with the sidebar; and an ASan browser_test that clears and re-sets the region's tab strip view (the ResetTabStrip path), then opens a tab and switches Space, passes with no use-after-free report.
- [ ] **WIN-2** (P0) Rebuild the screenshot capture lifetimes in screenshot_capture.cc. Compute the file name and the download directory before any asynchronous step, and bind values plus a WeakPtr<WebContents> instead of raw WebContents* and Profile*. Return "Other" from FullPageCapturer::GetTypeForMetrics, give it a timeout of about 15 s, and start it in two phases (no delete-this inside the constructor). Keep one capture per WebContents (user data) and ignore a second start. Write every file on one SequencedTaskRunner with exclusive create (base::GetUniquePath or FLAG_CREATE with retry), with the host sanitised for the file system. Fixes: WIN-F4, WIN-F26, WIN-F33, L56, L58, L61, L62. Done when: an ASan browser_test runs ⇧⌘2 and closes the tab in the same task, and does the same with a private window closed mid-capture, with no report; ⇧⌘1 in a build from tooling/args/debug.gn saves a file with no DCHECK; ⇧⌘2 pressed twice within one second leaves two distinct files in Downloads; ⌥⇧⌘2 pressed twice shows one dimming overlay, and one Escape removes it. **Status 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** two ⇧⌘2 within a second leave two files (`ScreenshotCaptureTest.TwoQuickCapturesLeaveTwoFiles`: the second waits its turn); the ASan and debug-build parts are open.
- [ ] **WIN-3** (P0) Decide the rail form from the drawn width in VerticalTabStripRegionView::Layout (width() at or below the collapsed width plus a margin, as TabView::CollapsedWidth does), calling ApplyRailForm only when the answer changes. Remove the calls from UpdateInteriorMargin and AnimateExpandOnHover. Install the hover-expand click handler at most once (a tracked flag or a scoped helper) and remove it in AnimateExpandOnHover and ResetExpandOnHoverTimers. In rail form, give the Archived and New Tab rows a localized accessible name and tooltip. Fixes: WIN-F14, WIN-F15, L33, L34, L35, L36. Done when: an interactive_ui_test collapses and expands the sidebar with ⌘S and hover-expands the rail, stepping the animation, and at each step asserts that rows show labels exactly when the strip is wider than the rail; the same test hover-expands the rail ten times and finds one pre-target handler; with VoiceOver on and the sidebar collapsed, the two rows read "Archived tabs" and "New Tab"; and the owner watches ⌘S and a hover-expand in the running build and reports no flash of the wrong form. **Status 2026-09-29, patch 0057:** the browser_test half passes: `SidebarRailTest.TheFormFollowsTheDrawnWidth` (labels exactly when the strip is wider than the rail, at every step of ⌘S's collapse and expand), `SidebarRailTest.HoveringTheRailLeavesOneClickHandler` (ten hover-expands, one handler while waiting, none at rest) and `SidebarRailTest.TheRailsRowsKeepTheirNames` (the rail's rows are named "New Tab" and "Archived tabs"; sidebar Y15). Open: the names heard with VoiceOver on, "Archived tabs" localized (it is an English literal, like the row's "Archived"), and the owner's look at ⌘S and a hover-expand on the running build.
- [x] **WIN-4** (P0) Decide "private" in one place: add stedding::IsPrivateWindow(profile) = IsIncognitoProfile() and use it in SteddingWindowBackground, BrowserView::GetWindowTitle and the region view. Attach a Stedding-owned supplier for incognito windows in BrowserWidget::GetColorProviderKey, and delete IsPrivateColorKey's dark+grayscale inference. Carry the Space tint the same way, so user_color and installed themes are left alone (or document in spaces.md that Spaces replace themes). Decide in private.md what a Guest window shows. Fixes: WIN-F8, L6, L19, L7. Done when: a browser_test builds the full mixer chain for an incognito window, a normal dark window with the Grey colour, and a Guest window, and asserts graphite only for incognito; and in the running browser, choosing Grey in Customize Chrome in dark mode leaves the normal window's sidebar navy, with no "Private" label. **Proven 2026-09-29, patch 0057:** `PrivateCoatTest.OnlyAnIncognitoWindowIsGraphite` reads the ground of three real windows through their whole colour pipeline: graphite for the incognito window, which says "Private" in its title and title row; not for a normal window after `SetBrowserColorScheme(kDark)` and `SetIsGrayscale(true)` (Customize Chrome's Grey; with the old dark-and-grayscale inference put back, it fails there); not for a Guest window, which says nothing of "Private". On the running build, `tooling/capture-state --seed grey-dark` (the two preferences Customize Chrome writes) shows the normal window's sidebar navy with no "Private" label, `--incognito` shows graphite with "Private", and `--guest` the navy coat with no title row. Spaces and themes, documented rather than carried apart: spaces B31 now says the tint rides in Chromium's user-colour slot, so Spaces replace Customize Chrome's colour while a tint is on, and an installed theme still wins.
- [ ] **WIN-5** (P0) Stop copies from leaking tracking data and private-window content. In copy_link.cc, read the page's own title from the last committed NavigationEntry and fall back to the cleaned URL's host. Mark the Markdown and screenshot clipboard writes off the record for off-the-record profiles. Fixes: WIN-F21, WIN-F34. Done when: a browser_test navigates to an untitled page at a URL with utm_source and fbclid, runs IDC_STEDDING_COPY_MARKDOWN_LINK, and reads a clipboard label with no query; and on Windows, a Markdown copy and a screenshot taken in a private window do not appear in Win+V clipboard history. **Status 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** `CopyLinkTest.AMarkdownLinkDropsTheTrackers` passes; the Windows clipboard-history part needs a Windows build.
- [ ] **WIN-6** (P1) Own AddressRow and PageThemeColorController per TYPE_NORMAL window, off the record included, as their own BrowserWindowFeatures members rather than SpaceModel members. Install the sidebar density observer once at startup. Enable IDC_STEDDING_TOGGLE_ADDRESS_ROW only where AddressRow::From() is non-null. Record the private-window behaviour in toolbar.md and private.md. Fixes: WIN-F9, WIN-F18, L10, L43. Done when: in a private window, ⌘S hides the address row (with the setting on), ⇧⌘D brings it back, and the row takes the page colour on github.com; and a session started with --incognito uses the density chosen in Settings → Stedding.
- [ ] **WIN-7** (P1) Make AddressRow a passive rule. Add a full-screen signal to AddressRowSignals that keeps the row in immersive full screen. Clear the ⇧⌘D override from a collapse-state subscription, not inside IsVisible. Feed "caption buttons over the row" from the frame's layout. Observe the active tab's navigations for the localhost rule. Cache the last answer and post an InvalidateLayout only when it changes, instead of calling ToolbarSizeChanged on every active-tab change. Fixes: WIN-F10, WIN-F20, WIN-F29, WIN-F32, WIN-F37, L17, L18, L44, L45. Done when: an interactive_ui_test collapses the sidebar, enters ⌃⌘F, reveals the top edge and finds the location bar visible and focusable; the same test navigates the active tab to http://localhost:8000 and back, and asserts the row's visibility after each commit with no other input; a counter on BrowserView::Layout shows one layout per plain tab click, not three; and on Windows (tooling/win/capture.ps1), the first layout of a collapsed window shows the row with no flash.
- [ ] **WIN-8** (P1) Rewrite the page-colour plumbing. Paint the exact colour from PageThemeColorController wherever the bar shows: the row, the focused LocationBarView background, the omnibox popup and the separator. Keep only two process-wide suppliers (light bar and dark bar) on the ColorProviderKey, and re-theme only when the contrast side flips; otherwise SchedulePaint. Record which WebContents the colour came from, so the keep-while-loading rule applies only to a reload of the same tab. Replace closing_all() with a real closing check. Install SteddingWindowBackground once, and move the colour subscription out of it. Fixes: WIN-F5, WIN-F12, WIN-F23, WIN-F24, WIN-F30, L24, L65, L66, L67. Done when: in the running browser on a white page, focusing the address field shows a box and a dropdown whose sampled pixels are #FFFFFF (#0D1117 on GitHub dark); an interactive_ui_test loads a page that changes its background ten times and counts no more than one BrowserWidget::ThemeChanged per contrast flip; switching from a dark tab to a sleeping tab shows the ground, not the dark colour, while it loads; and the ColorProviderManager cache size stays constant across 50 page colours.
- [ ] **WIN-9** (P1) Paint a surface behind the revealed address row in macOS immersive full screen. First confirm: on a Mac build in light mode, open github.com, press ⌃⌘F, reveal the toolbar with tooling/capture-state, and sample the pixels behind and on the back and reload icons and the URL; then repeat in dark mode on a white page. If it is confirmed, install a Stedding background on the top container when it is parented to the overlay widget, painting the same bar from PageThemeColorController, and re-theme the overlay on a colour change. Inset the overlay toolbar by the strip's column width, not its hover-expanded width. Stop SteddingBrowserViewLayout from mutating views inside a const layout and from reading params after std::move. This is BACKLOG S-63. Fixes: WIN-F28, WIN-F38, L20, L21, L22. Done when: the same full-screen captures show the row in the page colour with contrasting icons on both pages and after a tab switch; and hovering the rail in full screen does not move the back button.
- [ ] **WIN-10** (P1) Make the empty address row drag the window. In the Stedding hunk of ToolbarView::IsPositionInWindowCaption, return true for points inside the visible centring spacers. Fixes: WIN-F2. Done when: an interactive_ui_test hit-tests a point in the left spacer and gets HTCAPTION; and in the running browser, dragging from the empty row moves the window, and double-clicking it zooms it on macOS.
- [ ] **WIN-11** (P1) Hide the ⌘-held row numbers when the window stops being active: in the paint-as-active callback, stop cmd_hold_timer_ and call HideNumberBadges(), and make ShowNumberBadges refuse to show on an inactive widget. Fixes: WIN-F13, L31. Done when: an interactive_ui_test presses ⌘, deactivates the widget before the key-up and waits 300 ms, then finds no badges; and in the running browser, holding ⌘, pressing Tab to another app and coming back shows no numbers.
- [ ] **WIN-12** (P1) Make the two-finger swipe switch Spaces on Windows and Linux: in VerticalTabStripFocusSwipeController::OnMouseEvent, use the Mac guard (return only when there is no on_swipe_ and kTabGroupsFocusing is off). Fixes: WIN-F16. Done when: on the Windows build, a horizontal two-finger swipe over the sidebar changes Space; and a test feeding horizontal wheel events to a controller built with an on_swipe callback sees the callback run.
- [ ] **WIN-13** (P1) Copy only what the user chose, and write correct Markdown. In CopyLink, drop the active tab's split partner unless the selection also holds a tab outside that split, and skip every IsTabHidden index. In MarkdownFor, escape the backslash first, then both brackets, and wrap the destination in <…> or percent-encode parentheses and spaces. Fixes: WIN-F3, WIN-F22, L51. Done when: a browser_test with the active tab in a split runs ⇧⌘C and reads one URL; a sidebar shift-click range across a collapsed folder copies only visible tabs; and ⌥⇧⌘C on a page titled "[JIRA-123] Fix login" pastes into a CommonMark renderer as one link over the whole title.
- [ ] **WIN-14** (P1) Move the screenshot codec work off the UI thread and report failures. Do the base64 decode, the PNG decode and encode, and the write in one sequenced ThreadPool task, and post only the clipboard write back. Cap the full-page area and say so when a capture is cropped. Show a failure toast on every early return. Carry the saved path in the toast's own action, not a process-global. Use platform wording ("Show in folder" on Windows) and name the real folder. Fixes: WIN-F25, L57, L59, L60. Done when: ⇧⌘1 on a 30,000 px page leaves the browser responsive (an interactive_ui_test measures no UI-thread task over 100 ms) and either saves or shows the cropped or failed toast; ⇧⌘2 on a crashed tab shows a failure toast; and after captures in two windows, each toast's button reveals its own file.
- [ ] **WIN-15** (P1) Make the bookmarks-to-pins conversion a one-time default: at conversion set kShowBookmarkBar false, and drop the ShouldShowBookmarkBar and star overrides, or add a Settings → Stedding toggle that clears kBookmarksConverted and have BookmarkBarController observe it. Fixes: WIN-F11, L1. Done when: after "bookmarks to pins" in the running browser, ⌘⇧B shows the bookmark bar and the star appears on a page; and a browser_test runs the conversion, then IDC_SHOW_BOOKMARK_BAR, and finds the bar visible.
- [ ] **WIN-16** (P2) Bind the sidebar downloads button to the action item (SetActionViewInterface or an ActionViewController), so its image, tooltip and enabled state follow UpdateIcon. Give its ring and badge sidebar colour ids. Use the anchor only when it IsDrawn(), falling back to the toolbar path. Push the anchor through SetSteddingAnchorView instead of the _for_testing lookup, and clear it at teardown. Fixes: WIN-F6, WIN-F7, L13. Done when: in the running browser during a download, the sidebar button shows the in-progress icon, and its tooltip and VoiceOver name say how many downloads are in progress; on a dark page in light mode the dormant ring is dark on the sand sidebar; and an interactive_ui_test with the region hidden shows the toolbar button and anchors the bubble to it.
- [ ] **WIN-17** (P2) Replace the tests that cannot fail. Rename or fix ClosingEveryTabReThemesAfterwards and ToggleOverridesUntilTheSidebarChanges. Test the supplier through controller->bar_supplier(), and the untitled label with NavigateAndCommit. Build the full mixer chain for the inactive-row and private tests. Paint a BrowserView with the top container in a second widget (T24). Add a screenshot test target. Update or disable, with a Stedding comment, the upstream system_menu_model_builder browser tests that assert removed menus, and add the frame/ browser_tests to the release sweep. Fold address_row_rules into address_row and test it through BrowserView. Fixes: WIN-F17, WIN-F27, L25, L46. Done when: deliberately reverting each of the WIN-1, WIN-3, WIN-5, WIN-7, WIN-8, WIN-11 and WIN-13 fixes turns at least one browser_test or interactive_ui_test red; and the frame/ browser_tests are green in the sweep.
- [ ] **WIN-18** (P2) Capture the page being looked at: resolve the screenshot target through the helper PeekView uses for Copy Link, and register the capture chords on PeekView. Fixes: WIN-F35. Done when: with a peek open in the running browser, ⇧⌘2 saves the peek's page, and ⌥⇧⌘2 draws its overlay over the peek.
- [ ] **WIN-19** (P2) Extend the tracking table and spec L2 with srsltid, gbraid, wbraid, gclsrc, _gl, ttclid, igsh and li_fat_id, add si on open.spotify.com, and delete HasTrackingParameters and WriteLinkToClipboard (or use them). Fixes: WIN-F36, L47, L48, L49, L50. Done when: in the running browser, ⇧⌘C on a Google Shopping result link and on a Spotify share link copies the URL without those parameters, and the toast says tracking was removed.
- [ ] **WIN-20** (P2) Settle the Space slide. Either drive a translate of the Space's title and list from the swipe's cumulative offset, commit or spring back on release, and defer the tint re-theme to the animation's end; or write into spaces B32 that the swipe does not track. Fixes: WIN-F31. Done when: the owner, in the running build with Reduce Motion off, swipes between Spaces and accepts the result, or the spec row says what ships; and an interactive_ui_test switching Space with ⌃2 observes a running layer animation on the list.
- [ ] **WIN-21** (P2) Limit the card inset, the transparent mat and SteddingWindowBackground to TYPE_NORMAL windows, the same condition BrowserViewLayout::CreateLayout uses. Fixes: L23. Done when: an OAuth popup, an installed PWA and undocked DevTools open in the running browser with square, edge-to-edge content and no Stedding gutter.
- [ ] **WIN-22** (P2) Keep the status pill and the find bar inside the card. Cap the pill's mouse avoidance at the inset (or switch sides), mirror it under RTL, and inset the find bar by gfx::scrollbar_size() with its immersive branch kept. Fixes: L14, L15, L16, L37, L38. Done when: in the running browser with "Show scroll bars: Always", ⌘F leaves the scrollbar's top clickable; and moving the pointer onto a link's pill keeps it inside the card's bottom edge.
- [ ] **WIN-23** (P2) Define the short-menu rows once, as feature pairs mapped to their page-menu and app-menu ids. Add Translate, Compose and send-to-devices. Apply the list to main_menu_builder.mm, and point Help at stedding.dev or drop it. Update M2 and M3. Fixes: L52, L53, L54, L55. Done when: with short menus on, the running browser's page menu, app menu and Mac menu bar show no Lens, AI Mode, QR, Reading mode or Translate rows, and Help does not open support.google.com.
- [ ] **WIN-24** (P3) Move the hard-coded English in the frame, the sidebar, the window title, Space names and the screenshot row labels into stedding_strings.grdp, with one name per capture row across the menu, the command bar and the shortcut reference. Fixes: L28, L29, L30, L69, L41. Done when: launched with --lang=de, the sidebar rows, the private window title and the capture rows in the menu and ⌘T are in German.
- [ ] **WIN-25** (P3) Make kArcStyleWindow an honest switch. Either gate each layout constant on it or say in stedding_ui_metrics.h that it cannot be disabled and drop the per-site checks. Move the drift_tabs, folder_tabs and other capture params behind a switch honoured only in non-official builds. Fixes: L8, L9, L11, L12. Done when: an official build started with --enable-features=SteddingArcStyleWindow:drift_tabs/5 leaves the user's tabs untouched; and tooling/capture-state still recreates its states in a dev build.
- [ ] **WIN-26** (P3) Shrink the upstream surface. Delete the orphan SpaceModel and its accessor from EmbedderBrowserWindowFeatures, and move SpaceModel, TabArchiver, FolderSessionService (and WIN-6's per-window owners) into the embedder hook's Init and TearDown. Move the sidebar extras out of vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc into a Stedding-owned view or controller. Revert brace-only hunks, and keep one table per chord. Fixes: L2, L3, L4, L5, L27, L41. Done when: tooling/dev status shows the two embedder files restored to upstream; and a browser_test opening a popup, a PWA and DevTools finds no SpaceModel on them while the normal window's Spaces work.
- [ ] **WIN-27** (P3) Make comments and spec status lines true. Fix the ownership and lifetime notes on the suppliers, the drag-onto-Space default, the hover-delay "Chromium's default" label, the class comment on SteddingWindowBackground and the BUILD.gn comments. Remove the VLOGs. Share one sidebar row-button class, call the direct base's OnThemeChanged, and regenerate the status lines of toolbar.md, copy-link.md, screenshot.md and page.md from their rows (T4's example.com probe included). Fixes: L2, L26, L32, L40, L42, L64, L68, L70. Done when: `tooling/check-repo` passes, and each spec's status line matches the count of its built, gap and model-only rows.
- [ ] **WIN-28** (P3) Stop the About line naming a release the build is not: bump VERSION to a -dev suffix whenever the pin or the series moves past a tag, or append the series commit. Fixes: L39, L63. Done when: chrome://settings/help in a build of the current series does not read "0.2.0-beta.8".

#### Findings

| ID | Sev | State | Where (path:line, patch) | Problem | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WIN-F1 | critical | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc:1151, space_tab_visibility_controller.h:92; 0004 (FolderDragTarget 0008) | "Switch to horizontal tabs" (More tools, Mac View menu) destroys the TabStripView while SpaceTabVisibilityController (raw_ref) and FolderDragTarget still point at it. The next tab insert or Space switch dereferences freed memory. | Reset both in OnTabStripViewWillClear, and remove horizontal mode under kArcStyleWindow. |
| WIN-F2 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/toolbar/toolbar_view.cc:1646; 0002, 0037 | The plain-View centring spacers count as interactive children, so the empty address row hit-tests as HTCLIENT: no window drag and no double-click zoom from it. | Treat points in the visible spacers as caption in ToolbarView::IsPositionInWindowCaption. |
| WIN-F3 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/copy_link.cc:127; 0019 | CopyLink copies every selected tab. Upstream adds the split partner to every selection, and range selection ignores IsTabHidden, so split view copies both panes and ranges copy hidden tabs. | Build the list from the explicit selection, drop the split partner, and skip hidden indices. |
| WIN-F4 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/screenshot/screenshot_capture.cc:319; 0014, 0017 | The visible capture binds raw WebContents* and Profile* into the asynchronous surface-copy callback. A tab or private window closed mid-copy gives a use-after-free in Deliver and SavePng. | Compute the file name and directory up front, and bind values plus a WeakPtr. |
| WIN-F5 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/theme/page_theme_color_controller.cc:49; 0017, 0039 | The supplier is built from the colour masked with 0xFFF0F0F0, so the focused field, dropdown and separator are not the page colour (#F0F0F0 on white). The unit test bypasses the mask. | Paint the exact colour directly, keep the supplier for foreground only, and test through bar_supplier(). |
| WIN-F6 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/download/bubble/download_toolbar_ui_controller.cc:483; 0013, 0021 | The sidebar downloads button counts as present whenever it exists. With the sidebar undrawn, the toolbar button is suppressed and the bubble anchors to an off-screen view. | Use the anchor only when IsDrawn(), otherwise fall back to the toolbar, and push and clear the anchor properly. |
| WIN-F7 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/vertical/vertical_tab_strip_bottom_container.cc:61; 0013 | The sidebar button copies the action's image once and has a hard-coded tooltip. It never shows the active or in-progress state, and its ring colours follow the page bar. | Bind the button to the action item, and use sidebar colour ids and a localized string. |
| WIN-F8 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/color/stedding_color_mixer.cc:37; 0034 | "Private" is decided three ways. A normal dark window with the Grey colour is painted graphite, and Guest is labelled Private without the coat. | One IsPrivateWindow helper (IsIncognitoProfile) plus an explicit supplier on the key. |
| WIN-F9 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:104; 0034 (0013, 0029, 0031) | PageThemeColorController, AddressRow and the density hook are built inside SpaceModel, which private windows do not get. There is no page colour, ⌘S row hiding or ⇧⌘D there, and a private-only session ignores density. | Own them per TYPE_NORMAL window, and install density at startup. |
| WIN-F10 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_view.cc:3073; 0029, 0039 | With the sidebar collapsed, IsToolbarVisible returns false before the immersive logic, so the full-screen reveal has no toolbar, contrary to T8. | Add a full-screen signal that forces the row on. |
| WIN-F11 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/bookmarks/bookmark_bar_controller.cc:231; 0032 | After "bookmarks to pins", the bar and star are off for good. Show Bookmarks Bar flips its check mark and does nothing, and open windows do not react. | Make the conversion a one-time default, or add a reset and observe the pref. |
| WIN-F12 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_widget.cc:517; 0017 | The page colour rides the window's ColorProviderKey. Each change re-themes the whole window synchronously and caches another provider. | Two process-wide contrast suppliers; paint the exact colour directly. |
| WIN-F13 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc:565; 0019 | The ⌘-held numbers stay after the window loses focus with ⌘ down, because deactivation neither stops the timer nor hides the badges. | Hide the badges and stop the timer on deactivation, and refuse to show them on an inactive widget. |
| WIN-F14 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc:1256; 0039 | The rail form and the address row follow the collapse state at the animation's start or end, not the drawn width, so every collapse, expand and hover-expand draws the wrong form. | Decide the form in Layout from width(). |
| WIN-F15 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc:1598; 0039 | In rail form, SetText("") clears the accessible name of the Archived and New Tab rows. The labels are hard-coded English. | Set an explicit localized name and tooltip in rail form. |
| WIN-F16 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/vertical/vertical_tab_strip_focus_swipe_controller.cc:39; 0013 | The Windows and Linux OnMouseEvent path still requires kTabGroupsFocusing, which is off in the only configuration that installs the Space callback, so the swipe never fires. | Use the Mac guard. |
| WIN-F17 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/stedding_window_background_unittest.cc:20; 0013, 0034, 0037, 0042, 0052 | The tests exercise pure helpers or lone mixers. None covers the UAF, the rail form, the hover delay, the badges, the tint or the hit test. | Add view-level and browser tests on the real paths. |
| WIN-F18 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:106; 0029 | AddressRow is absent in private and guest windows, but IDC_STEDDING_TOGGLE_ADDRESS_ROW stays enabled and silently does nothing. ⌘S never hides the row there. | Own AddressRow per window, and disable the command where it is null. |
| WIN-F19 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/address_row.cc:44; 0029 | The collapse rule ignores whether tabs are vertical. Collapse and then switching to horizontal leaves a window with no toolbar that ⌘S cannot restore. | Require ShouldDisplayVerticalTabs(), or drop horizontal mode. |
| WIN-F20 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_view.cc:3073; 0029, 0039 | Same root as WIN-F10: AddressRowSignals has no full-screen input, so the immersive reveal has no URL field or back button. | Add an immersive signal to the rules, and test it. |
| WIN-F21 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/copy_link.cc:32; 0016 | GetTitle() is never empty in production (it falls back to the formatted URL), so an untitled page's Markdown label carries the tracking query that was just stripped. | Read the committed entry's own title, and fall back to the cleaned host. |
| WIN-F22 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/copy_link.cc:69; 0016, 0019 | MarkdownFor escapes only "]", which unbalances bracketed titles. A trailing backslash or ")" in the URL breaks the link. The test pins the broken output. | Escape backslash and both brackets, wrap or encode the destination, and fix the test. |
| WIN-F23 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/theme/page_theme_color_controller.cc:122; 0039 | The reload keep-colour rule also keeps the previous tab's colour on a newly activated tab that is still loading. | Apply the rule only to the WebContents the colour came from. |
| WIN-F24 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/theme/page_theme_color_controller.cc:72; 0017 | Every exact colour change re-themes the whole window, even when the supplier is unchanged. Providers accumulate without eviction, keyed by pointers that dangle after close. | Notify only when the supplier changes, otherwise repaint; bound the suppliers. |
| WIN-F25 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/screenshot/screenshot_capture.cc:131; 0014 | PNG encode and decode, JSON and base64 run on the UI thread with no size cap, freezing on long pages. Every failure returns silently. | Do the codec and file work on a sequenced pool task, cap the area, and add a failure toast. |
| WIN-F26 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/screenshot/screenshot_capture.cc:227; 0014 | GetTypeForMetrics returns "Stedding", which DCHECKs in debug builds on every ⇧⌘1. There is no timeout, and delete-this runs inside the constructor. | Return "Other", add a timer, and use two-phase start. |
| WIN-F27 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/address_row_unittest.cc:24; 0016, 0029, 0037 | The window-level tests do not exercise what their names claim: no sidebar change, an inverted assertion, the mask bypassed, a faked GetTitle. There are no screenshot tests. | Rewrite them as BrowserWithTestWindowTest cases on the real paths. |
| WIN-F28 | medium | plausible | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/layout/stedding_browser_view_layout.cc:211; 0037 | In immersive full screen nothing paints behind the revealed row, while its icons keep the page bar's contrast colour. Confirm with the full-screen capture in WIN-9. | Install a Stedding background on the top container in the overlay (S-63). |
| WIN-F29 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/address_row.cc:76; 0029 | Every active-tab change runs a synchronous ToolbarSizeChanged, whether or not the row's visibility changed. | Cache the answer, and invalidate only on a change. |
| WIN-F30 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_view.cc:6101; 0013 | SteddingWindowBackground is rebuilt and re-subscribed on every theme and activation change, including from inside its own callback. | Set it once, and move the subscription to a per-window object. |
| WIN-F31 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/vertical_tab_strip_region_view.cc:1170; 0052 | The Space slide moves only the unpinned list after the switch, with no gesture tracking. It matches B32 as written, but not Arc. | Track the swipe, or write the limitation into B32. |
| WIN-F32 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/address_row.cc:76; 0029 | Same root as WIN-F29. It also forces a synchronous layout on in-split tab changes, where upstream deliberately lays out asynchronously. | Post an InvalidateLayout only when the answer changes. |
| WIN-F33 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/screenshot/screenshot_capture.cc:80; 0014, 0017 | The file name is chosen by check-then-write on an unsequenced pool, so two same-second captures can overwrite each other, and the 100th collision overwrites. | Write on one sequence with exclusive create. |
| WIN-F34 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/copy_link.cc:101; 0014, 0016 | Stedding's Markdown and screenshot clipboard writes from private windows are not marked off the record (on Windows they land in clipboard history). | Call MarkAsOffTheRecord for off-the-record profiles. |
| WIN-F35 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/screenshot/screenshot_capture.cc:311; 0014 | Screenshots capture the tab behind an open peek, while Copy Link in a peek copies the peek. | Resolve the target through the peek-aware helper. |
| WIN-F36 | low | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/clean_link.cc:21; 0016 | The tracker table (as spec L2 lists it) misses srsltid, gbraid, wbraid, gclsrc, _gl, ttclid, igsh, li_fat_id and Spotify's si. | Extend the table and the spec. |
| WIN-F37 | low | plausible | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/address_row.cc:50; 0029 | There is no navigation observer for the localhost rule, and the override resets inside a layout-time getter. Confirm whether a same-tab navigation relayouts (breakpoint on IsToolbarVisible after commit). | Observe navigations, and clear the override from a collapse subscription. |
| WIN-F38 | low | plausible | chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_widget.cc:512; 0017 | The overlay inherits the page-bar key, so full-screen icons may contrast with a bar that is not painted there, and may keep the old tab's colours. Confirm with the capture in WIN-9. | Paint the bar in the overlay, or drop the supplier there, and re-theme owned widgets. |

### Command bar, commands, menus, peek and links

This area works on the happy path in a normal Mac window and breaks almost everywhere else. The command bar opens, classifies typed text through the omnibox's own classifier, runs keyword searches, and switches between tabs mode and actions mode as the spec describes. Its list layer, though, is broken. Enter can run a different row from the highlighted one. The list never scrolls to the selection. Tab rows hold raw strip indices that can activate the wrong tab or crash. Search matches titles only, with no ranking. ⌘L followed by Enter duplicates the tab instead of navigating it. Actions mode lists Chromium's whole action registry and runs each row with an empty context, so typing "Ask" or "Pin" and pressing Enter kills the browser. Menus and keys are solid in normal windows, but four edges are broken. The tab menu crashes in horizontal-tab mode, which is one click away in the app menu. Clear can close the whole window, and in a private window it always does when every tab is unpinned. The Space chords do nothing in private, popup and web-app windows, and Chromium's own function for those chords is gone there too. Nearly every little-window key is dead. Peek shows no page to anyone: the page is laid out 0 px tall. Even with that fixed, the peek is a bare WebContents that has no popup blocker, dialogs or permission prompts. It ignores independent-session cookie jars. It re-sends links as if the user had typed them, and a long page title can hide its origin. Routing into Spaces loses a typed address when the route crosses cookie jars. The tests in this area go through seams and helpers, not the real key, menu or navigation paths, so they stayed green through all of this, and the specs mark many broken rows as built. The reviewers found nothing here that explains tabs not selecting on click or the rail's look. The area does add real crash paths: the "Ask" and "Pin" rows, the horizontal tab menu, the stale tab index, and the CreateBrowserWindow CHECK. None of them is the PaintPageBar signature already on file. Nothing was run during the audit, so "confirmed" means traced in the code, not reproduced.

#### Features

| Feature | Real state | Why (finding ids) | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| ⌘T command bar: tab search, suggestions, archived rows (K1, K2, K4, K5, K7, archive A10) | Opens and classifies typed text correctly. Enter can open a different row from the highlighted one. The list never scrolls. Stale tab indices can crash the browser. Search is title-only, ASCII case-folding, first four in strip order, this window only. Focus is lost on close. Essentials jump to their origin Space. | F5, F7, F8, F9, F10, F11, F24, F30, F33, F34, F35, F63, F67 | **Fix.** Rewrite the list layer around one Row vector (CMD-5), then ranking, focus and GoTo. |
| ⌘L: edit the current address (K12) | The prefill works. Enter always opens a new tab. ⌥⌘F and Windows Ctrl+E / Ctrl+K still target an omnibox that cannot take focus while the address row is hidden. | F6, F12, F15, F37, L35 | **Fix.** |
| Actions mode: Stedding rows (K8–K11, K13–K17) | The rows exist and run. Escape shows stale rows. Rows the specs claim for pins H6, tabs R5 and tabs R14 are missing. The preference rows have drifted from the real prefs and show Mac glyphs on Windows. | F24, F29, F30, L7–L10 | **Fix.** Generate the preference rows from one table shared with Settings. |
| Actions mode: Chromium action-registry rows | Two rows crash the browser ("Ask", "Pin"). Others are no-ops, duplicates, or Google entry points the series removed elsewhere. One extension row can outlive its item. | F1, F61 | **Cut.** Remove the registry walk and replace it with a Stedding allow-list of IDC commands. Correct K9 and K15 to match. |
| One-shot rows: Turn Bookmarks into Pinned Tabs, Import from Arc, Back Up the Sidebar Now | Each runs on a single Enter, with no confirmation and no result shown. The conversion cannot be undone. | F25, L5 | **Simplify.** Confirm before converting, hide the row once the profile is converted, and show a result toast. |
| @Space filter (K19) and keyword search (K18) | The rows are filtered, but Enter web-searches the literal "@Work …". The keyword lookup is case-sensitive and accepts extension and starter-pack keywords. | F31, L17, L25 | **Fix.** |
| Hide Other Spaces in the bar (share coat Sc3) | Not implemented. The bar that toggles the coat still shows other Spaces' tabs and names. | F32 | **Fix.** |
| Space keys and menus (spaces B18–B23, B28, B29) | Work in normal windows. They are dead keys in private, popup and web-app windows, where Chromium's own function for the same chord is lost as well. The tab-traversal chords are reserved, so web editors never see them. | F38, F40, L38, L40, L45 | **Fix.** |
| Clear: ⇧⌘K and the Clear line (spaces B20, private V2) | Closes the window when every tab is unpinned. In a private window it takes the incognito session with it. | F14, F28 | **Fix (P0).** |
| Tab context menu in the sidebar (menus M1, M4, M5, M7) | Built and filtered by row kind. The order differs from M1, there is no Move to Folder for existing folders, and the labels are English literals. | F44, L52–L54, L58 | **Fix.** |
| Horizontal tab strip and "Switch to horizontal tabs" | The Stedding tab menu reaches a fatal NOTREACHED with two or more Spaces. The toggle, still in the app menu, the Mac View menu and the Windows system menu, turns the product off. | F2, F45 | **Cut.** Hide the toggle under kArcStyleWindow in all three menus, and range-guard the menu ids so the crash cannot recur. |
| Page and app menus without Google rows (M2, M3, M6) | Mostly done. The profile-submenu rows and the Mac Tab > Group Tab rows remain, and the separators are not collapsed. | F42, L34, L46, L49, L50, L57, L62 | **Fix.** Hide the Mac group rows. |
| Copy link (copy-link L1–L6) | Works. Markdown escapes only "]", and the menu paths ignore an open peek. | L39, L48 | **Fix** (small). |
| ⌘1–9 with row numbers (tabs R10, R11) | ⌘9 goes to a different row from the one the "9" badge marks. | F43 | **Fix.** |
| ⌘W sleeps pinned tabs | Correct in normal windows, but also takes over ⌘W in web-app windows. | F41 | **Fix.** |
| ⌃⇥ through recent tabs (switcher X1) | Works, but never falls back to Chromium's order. | L47 | **Fix.** |
| ⌘T and the New Tab row replacing the new tab page | No user path opens chrome://newtab, so extensions that override the new tab page are unreachable from ⌘T. | F39 | **Fix.** |
| Mac pane focus on F6 (spaces B19, shortcuts Z2) | Probably dead: moved to a bare F-key in a table that only modified keys reach. Needs a device check. | F62, L30, L31 | **Fix** after the check. |
| Shortcut reference (shortcuts Z1–Z6) | Lists chords that never fire. Its tests are presence checks that the specs cite as proof that the keys work. | F46, L36 | **Fix.** |
| Peek (peek P1–P10) | Broken for everyone: the page is 0 px tall. The peek is a bare WebContents: no popup blocker, dialogs, file chooser or permissions. It uses the shared jar from isolated Spaces, re-sends links as if typed, and lets the page title hide the origin. target=_blank and window.open never peek. The overlay outlives tab and Space switches. | F3, F4, F20–F23, F52–F60, F66 | **Fix** by rebuilding the host. Ship it off by default until CMD-9 and CMD-10 land. |
| Little window for links from other apps (little E1–E5) | Mac only. It opens and respects routes. Only ⌘O works. It skips upstream's profile and untrusted-launch checks, and the little mark is lost on restore. | F13, F16, F17, F18, F27, F47, F48, F49, F65, L67, L68, L76–L78 | **Simplify.** Keep the window, ⌘O, ⇧⌘O and Escape, and fix them. Cut ⌃1–9 in little windows and the "Open in <Space>" rows: remove the code and withdraw those parts of E1, E2 and E5. |
| Routing into Spaces (routing D2, D3, D6) | Works within one jar. A typed address routed across jars is lost. The first request of a routed new tab, and every link opened in a new window, uses the wrong jar. Drags and promotions are re-routed. D3 works on the Mac only. | F19, F50, F51, L72–L75 | **Fix.** |

#### Work, in order

- [ ] **CMD-1** (P0) Replace actions mode's walk of Chromium's action registry with a Stedding-owned allow-list: IDC commands run through the existing `command()` helper, which checks IsCommandEnabled. List no page-action or pin/unpin verbs, and bind nothing by raw ActionItem pointer. Fixes: F1, F61. Done when: in a normal and in a private window, ⇧⌘P then "Ask", "Pin" and "Shopping" shows no crashing rows. A browser_test opens the bar with `chrome::ExecuteCommand(IDC_STEDDING_COMMAND_PALETTE)` and presses Enter on every listed row through the widget, skipping only rows on an explicit side-effect list, and the browser survives. For F61 (plausible), the ASan check in its row (an extension reload while the bar is open) shows no use-after-free. **Status 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** `CommandBarActionsTest.EveryRowRunsInANormalWindow` and `.EveryRowRunsInAPrivateWindow` pass (⇧⌘P, "Ask", "Shopping" and "Pin" list no page action; every row runs, bar the four the test names with its reasons); on the way the bar was found offering to turn Peek on, and no longer does. F61's ASan run is open.
- [x] **CMD-2** (P0) *(Superseded in part by decision 1: the switch stays in every menu; the range checks and the one helper below are the fix.)* Hide "Switch to horizontal tabs" under kArcStyleWindow in the app menu, the Mac View menu and the Windows system menu, and disable IDC_TOGGLE_VERTICAL_TABS. Range-check the Move-to-Space and Route-to-Space ids before TabStripModel's switch in IsContextMenuCommandEnabled and ExecuteContextMenuCommand. Also build the short menu only for the collection controller, or route its verbs through one Stedding helper that both strip controllers call. Fixes: F2, F45. Done when: none of the three menus shows the toggle. A browser_test that forces the horizontal strip with three Spaces right-clicks a tab through BrowserTabStripController, the menu builds with its submenus, and the browser does not crash. **Proven 2026-09-28, patch 0057,** with decision 1 in place of the hidden toggle: `SpaceSwitchTest.TheTabMenuWorksWithHorizontalTabs` builds the menu as a right click on the horizontal strip does, through BrowserTabStripController and a TabContextMenuController, with its submenus, and runs their third rows; the native menu itself is not shown.
- [ ] **CMD-3** (P0) Add one Stedding helper in space_commands.cc that inserts a New Tab (`AddTabAt(chrome://newtab)`, as pin_lifecycle.cc does) before Clear would close every tab in the strip. Call it from both the private branch and SpaceModel::ClearUnpinnedTabs, and never run IDC_NEW_TAB from code that needs a tab. Fixes: F14, F28. Done when: in a new profile with one Space and two unpinned tabs, ⇧⌘K leaves the window open with one New Tab. In a private window with two tabs, clicking the Clear line leaves the window and the incognito session open. browser_tests run both through `chrome::ExecuteCommand(IDC_STEDDING_CLEAR_SPACE)` and through the sidebar's Clear-line callback, and assert that the window is still open with one tab. **Status 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** ⇧⌘K keeps the window with one New Tab in a normal and a private window (`ClearSpaceTest.ClearingEveryTabKeepsTheWindow`, `.ClearingAPrivateWindowKeepsIt`), and a mouse press on the Clear line clears (`ClearSpaceTest.TheClearLineClosesTheLooseTabs`). The Clear line shows only between a pinned run and other tabs (spaces B17), so a window with no pins and a private window have none: the owner decides whether it should show there (Arc's behaviour to be checked first).
- [x] **CMD-4** (P0) Until CMD-9 and CMD-10 are done, register `stedding.peek.enabled` with default false (chrome/browser/ui/stedding/stedding_prefs.cc) and mark peek.md P1–P9 broken with this plan's ids. Fixes (interim): F3, F4, F21, F22, F23. Done when: in a fresh profile, a cross-site link in a pinned tab navigates the tab and no peek appears, and Settings → Stedding shows Peek off. **Proven 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** `DefaultsTest.ALinkFromAPinnedTabNavigatesIt` (a fresh profile); Settings shows no Peek row at all while Peek is rebuilt (settings T3), which replaces "shows Peek off"; peek.md P1–P9 cite CMD-9.
- [x] **CMD-5** (P0) Rewrite the command bar's list layer in command_bar_view.cc: one `std::vector<Row>`, each row with a typed target (typed text, `tabs::TabHandle`, archived SessionID, suggestion GURL, action) and the de-duplication applied once. That one vector drives painting, the arrow keys and Enter. Keep the row views between updates, restyle only the old and new chosen rows, and call ScrollViewToVisible on the chosen row. Observe the TabStripModel while the bar is open and resolve handles at run time, doing nothing if the tab is gone. Make SetMode(kTabs) refresh the results. Have OnThemeChanged only restyle. Classify once per query. Delete the dead Escape branch and the false comments. Fixes: F8, F9, F10, F24, F30, F33, F63, F67, L18, L21, L23, L24. It sends VKEY_DOWN n times and VKEY_RETURN through the widget, and the target that runs is the highlighted row's for every n. In actions mode, 15 Downs keep the highlighted row inside the scroll viewport. Closing a background tab while the bar is open, then Enter, activates the intended tab, or nothing if that tab closed. ⇧⌘P, "cap", Escape shows tab rows that match "cap". **Proven 2026-09-28, patch 0057,** by browser_tests in place of the interactive_ui_test, the keys through the field's own handler: `CommandBarKeysTest.TheHighlightedRowIsTheOneThatRuns` (⌘T with two archived rows and the omnibox's duplicate of the typed row dropped: Down n and Enter runs row n for every n), `.TheHighlightedRowStaysInView` (fifteen Downs in actions mode), `.ATabClosedUnderTheBar` (nothing if the chosen tab closed; the intended tab when another closed), `.EscapeFromActionsKeepsTheText`.

Done when: with two Spaces and Hide Other Spaces on, ⌘T with an empty query shows no tab title or Space name from the other Space. The Sc3 test `CommandBarViewTest.ShareCoatFiltersToTheActiveSpace` exists and opens the bar through `chrome::ExecuteCommand`. **Proven 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** `CommandBarViewTest.ShareCoatFiltersToTheActiveSpace` opens the bar through `chrome::ExecuteCommand`; `CommandBarTest.HidingOtherSpacesHidesThemFromTheBar` does the same in a real window: with Hide Other Spaces on, ⌘T lists this Space's tab and nothing of the other, in both modes.

A browser_test that opens an untrusted-launch StartupTab to an EmbeddedTestServer sees `Sec-Fetch-Site` other than `none` and no SameSite=Strict cookie on the request.

In a Stedding helper called from Navigate/CreateTargetContents, compute the route target (falling back to the source tab's Space, then the active Space), create the contents in that Space's partition, and tag them. Then route only tagged tabs in MaybeRouteInsertedTab, so drags, moves and peek promotions are never re-routed. Shift-clicking a link in isolated "Work" opens a window showing Work, and the request carries Work's cookie.
