In PromoteLittleWindow, fall back to NEW_FOREGROUND_TAB when the target's active tab is split, pinned or in another Space. Cut the ⌃1–9 little-window branch, the explicit-Space parameter and the command bar's "Open in <Space>" block, and remove those rows from the shortcut reference. Fixes: F13, F18, F27, F48, F65. Done when: in a window made by OpenLittleWindow, driven with real key events in an interactive_ui_test, Escape closes it, ⌘O moves the page into the last normal window, and ⇧⌘O pairs it in a split, or opens a tab when that window's active tab is already split, with no crash. The shortcut reference lists no little-window chord that does nothing.
- [ ] **CMD-16** (P1) Move the new-tab peek hook out of chrome::Navigate into a non-views Stedding target called from BrowserWebContentsDelegate::AddNewContents and OpenURLFromTab. Accept the `contents_to_insert` shape with `original_user_gesture`, keep excluding NEW_POPUP, and adopt the contents without a second load. Decide in peek.md P8 whether a background-tab request (⌘-click, middle-click) peeks; if it does, the peek must not take focus. Fixes: F20, F66, L69, L70, L71. Done when: in a pinned test app, a `target=_blank` link and a gesture-driven `window.open` open a peek and no tab. An OAuth-style `window.open` popup still opens a popup. ⌘-click behaves as P8 now says. `gn check` passes for the navigator target.
- [ ] **CMD-17** (P1) Make the peek follow the window. Observe the tab strip, the SpaceModel and contents_container's bounds. Close the peek on an active-tab change, a Space change or removal of the source tab. Close it only on a left press outside the card. Handle ⌘W as "close the peek". Call set_source_tab on every Show. Refocus the page on Close. Fixes: F55, F56, F57, L79. Done when: with a peek open, clicking its title keeps it open and clicking the scrim closes it. ⌃2 or a sidebar click on another tab closes it. ⌘W closes the peek and leaves the pinned tab awake. ⌘S and ⇧⌘D leave the scrim exactly over the content area.
- [ ] **CMD-18** (P1) Where a window has no Spaces, translate the remapped chords back to Chromium's commands (Bookmark This Tab, next and previous tab) through a helper in space_commands.cc. Bind the Arc tab-traversal chords to Stedding alias ids that are not reserved. Decide per chord whether sidebar and Space commands should be reserved, and record the decision in shortcuts.md. Refresh TOGGLE_SPACE_PIN and the Spaces menu pin row on active-tab changes, and enable TOGGLE_ADDRESS_ROW only where an AddressRow exists. Fixes: F38, F40, L38, L40, L45. Done when: in a private window, ⌘D opens the bookmark bubble and ⌥⌘→ selects the next tab. On a test page that logs keydown, ⌥⌘↓ (Ctrl+Alt+↓ on Windows) reaches the page. On an essential, "Pin Tab in This Space" is disabled in the Mac menu and the Windows app menu.
- [ ] **CMD-19** (P1) Confirm first: on the Mac build, press F6 and Shift-F6 (with Fn if media keys are on) from the page and from the sidebar. If focus does not move, bind pane focus to a chord AppKit delivers, or add F6 to the Mac views accelerator table, and remove the bare-key rows from the hidden-shortcut table. Correct B19 and the Z2 reference row. Fixes: F62, L30, L31. Done when: on a Mac with VoiceOver on, the documented pane-focus key moves focus page → toolbar → sidebar and back, and the shortcut reference names that key.
- [ ] **CMD-20** (P2) When the query has an @Space prefix, drop the typed row or put it last, select the first matching tab, and send only the rest of the query to Classify and autocomplete. Lowercase keywords before lookup, and skip extension, starter-pack and inactive engines. Fixes: F31, L17, L25. Done when: typing "@Work budget" and pressing Enter activates the first Work tab matching "budget", and no search opens. "GH rust" uses the GH engine. "@gemini" and "@hist foo" do not open Google or chrome://history pages.
- [ ] **CMD-21** (P2) Put Turn Bookmarks into Pinned Tabs and Import from Arc behind a confirmation ("Press Enter again to …"), and hide the conversion row once kBookmarksConverted is set. Report the results of the import and the backup in a toast, and open the shortcuts page with ShowSingletonTab. Fixes: F25, L5. Done when: one Enter on "Turn Bookmarks into Pinned Tabs" changes nothing and a second Enter converts. After a conversion the row is gone. Import from Arc and Back Up the Sidebar Now each show a toast with counts or the error, including the keychain-denied case.
- [ ] **CMD-22** (P2) Give the bar a dialog role and a name, the list kListBox, and each row kListBoxOption with a selected state. Point the field's active descendant at the chosen row. Build rows as a small view with an elided title, a fixed trailing label for Space, kind or chord, and a hover state that moves the selection. Depends on CMD-5. Fixes: F26, L11, L22. Done when: with VoiceOver on, ⌘T, typing and Down announce the chosen row's title and Space. Hovering a row highlights it. A long title no longer hides the Space name or the chord.
- [ ] **CMD-23** (P2) Make Enter on an empty bar, or a leading "New Tab" row, open chrome://newtab so that new-tab overrides apply. Give the bar its own command ids, and let IDC_NEW_TAB keep creating a tab for internal callers. Fixes: F39. Done when: with a new-tab override extension installed, ⌘T then Enter shows the extension's page. `chrome::ExecuteCommand(IDC_NEW_TAB)` from a test adds a tab.
- [ ] **CMD-24** (P2) Return kClose from `stedding::CloseActionFor` unless the window is TYPE_NORMAL, and treat kNoOp as kClose. Same change as TAB-25's rule for windows without a SpaceModel; land it there (Phase 1). Fixes: F41. Done when: in a tabbed web-app window, ⌘W on its pinned home tab closes it, and no chrome://newtab appears in the app window.
- [ ] **CMD-25** (P2) Make ⌘9 and the badges agree through one list in tab_numbers.cc. With more than nine visible rows, badge rows 1–8 and put the last visible tab in slot 9, unless the owner's ⌘9 decision (section 2) says the ninth row. Done with WIN-11 and TAB-30. Fixes: F43. Done when: with twelve visible rows, holding ⌘ shows "9" on the last row, and ⌘9 activates that row. A test compares `NumberedTabs()[8]` with the tab IDC_SELECT_LAST_TAB activates.
- [ ] **CMD-26** (P2) Bring the short tab menu in line with M1: add a "Move to Folder" submenu of the row's Space's folders, then New Folder, with its command range handled like Move to Space. Label a multi-selection "Always Open These Sites in". Move the literals to stedding_strings.grdp as plural ICU strings, and rewrite SteddingOrder from the spec. Otherwise correct M1 and mark it partial. Fixes: F44, L52, L53, L54, L58. Done when: right-clicking a tab offers the Space's existing folders and moving the tab into one works. In a non-English locale the menu is fully translated.
- [ ] **CMD-27** (P2) Hide Tab > Group Tab and its Option alternate under kArcStyleWindow in main_menu_builder.mm, or disable IDC_WINDOW_GROUP_TAB and IDC_GROUP_TARGET_TAB. Fixes: F42, L46. Done when: the Mac Tab menu shows no group rows, and no user path creates a tab group.
- [ ] **CMD-28** (P2) Fix the little window's edges. When there is no normal window, ⌘O creates one and promotes into it; it never falls through to Open File. Without an anchor window, centre the little window on the work area. Count a route in ShouldOpenLittle only if its Space exists, and prune routes when a Space is deleted. Persist the little mark in session data, or leave little windows out of restore. Fixes: F47, F49, L76, L77, L78. Done when: with every normal window closed, ⌘O in a little window opens a normal window holding the page, and no file picker appears. A link to a site whose route points at a deleted Space opens in a little window. After quitting with a little window open and relaunching, ⌘O still promotes it (or it is not restored).
- [ ] **CMD-29** (P2) Add WillRedirectRequest to PeekNavigationThrottle, so a user link that redirects cross-site from the pin's site is cancelled and peeked. Peek only when the target is cross-site from both the pin's home and the tab's committed URL. Fixes: F52, F54. Done when: in a pinned google.com tab, a google.com/url?q= link to another site opens a peek and the pinned tab stays put. A drifted pin on okta.com follows a same-site okta.com link in the tab.
- [ ] **CMD-30** (P2) Decide in peek.md P9 whether ⇧⌘O from an essential produces an essential. If it should not, fall back to PromoteToTab, or unpin the new pane, when the source is pinned. Test with a pinned source. Fixes: F58. Done when: ⇧⌘O on a peek from an essential gives the result P9 states, and the test uses a pinned source.
- [ ] **CMD-31** (P2) Build the peek's button labels from IDS_ strings plus `PeekPromoteAccelerator().GetShortcutText()` and the split accelerator's text. Give PeekView a dialog role and a name that includes the host, and move focus into it. Fixes: F59, L80, L81. Done when: the Windows build's peek buttons read Ctrl+O and Ctrl+Shift+O. NVDA and VoiceOver announce a dialog with the host when a peek opens.
- [ ] **CMD-32** (P2) Replace the seam tests with tests that drive the real paths. Command-bar tests open the bar through `chrome::ExecuteCommand` in a real BrowserView widget and send keys through it. Peek tests click real anchors in a pinned tab (InProcessBrowserTest), or use a MockNavigationHandle with a link click that asserts CANCEL_AND_IGNORE. The shortcut tests resolve each chord through the dispatcher and assert it is enabled in the window type the row names. Delete RouteFor's `peeking` parameter and cover D6 through chrome::Navigate. Add PeekNewTabTest to tooling/dev, and reset the opener override with a scoped resetter. Fixes: F36, F46, F60, L74, L75. Done when: each of these deliberate breaks turns a named test red: skipping the throttle's registration, reverting the CURRENT_TAB change, reverting the Row vector, disabling a little-window command, and swapping the peek and route hooks in Navigate.
- [ ] **CMD-33** (P2) Rewrite the spec rows from the code. Mark commandbar K5, K9, K10, K11, K12, K15 and K19, pins H6, tabs R5 and R14, peek P8 and P9, little E1, E2 and E5, the M-rows in menus.md, and the copy-link and shortcuts rows partial or broken with the CMD id that fixes each. Label routing D3 and little E1 macOS-only until Windows has them. Fixes: F29, L26, L61, L67, L82. Done when: a person who follows every row marked built in commandbar.md, peek.md, little.md, routing.md, menus.md, shortcuts.md and copy-link.md sees that behaviour in the running browser on the stated platform.
- [ ] **CMD-34** (P3) Clamp the bar's width to the content width minus the gutters and its height to the space below its top edge, and re-centre it on contents_container bounds changes. Fixes: L12, L13, L14, L15. Done when: in a 600 DIP-wide window and in a short window, the bar stays inside the content area, and ⌘S or a rail expansion with the bar open keeps it centred.
- [ ] **CMD-35** (P3) Generate the bar's preference rows and the Settings options from one C++ table (key, IDS_ label, choices), with platform chord text. Fixes: L7, L8, L9, L10. Done when: on Windows the rows read Ctrl, not ⌘. Every stedding.* preference named in K9 has a row. A value added in Settings shows its label in the bar, not "?".
- [ ] **CMD-36** (P3) Fix the command target's GN dependencies (a registration hook, or documented nogncheck). Look chords up through `BrowserView::GetAccelerator` so they survive fullscreen. Add the Independent Session row only when a registry is set. Fixes: L2, L4, L6. Done when: `gn check` passes for the commandbar target. In macOS fullscreen, ⇧⌘P shows the same chords as in a window. A Blank Window's bar has no Independent Session row.
- [ ] **CMD-37** (P3) Move every Stedding IDC (IDC_SPACES_MENU included) into one documented reserved range in stedding_command_ids.h. Number Stedding toasts away from ToastId's growth front, and add them to enums.xml and histograms.xml. Fixes: L27, L28, L55, L56. Done when: the histogram presubmit and enum-consistency checks pass, and no Stedding id sits next to upstream's last allocated value.
- [ ] **CMD-38** (P3) Revert the formatting-only hunks in render_view_context_menu.cc and its unittest, global_keyboard_shortcuts_mac.mm and app_controller_mac.mm. Reduce app_controller_mac.mm's functional change to one call into a Stedding helper. Fixes: L32, L33, L63, L64, L65, L66. Done when: `git diff` of the pin against HEAD for those files shows only behaviour lines, and the patch series still applies and builds.
- [ ] **CMD-39** (P3) Clean up the command plumbing. Delete the four accelerators_cocoa.mm rows that have no menu item. Remove the duplicate enable and the test-only hook in the BrowserCommandController constructor, moving the capture trigger into tooling. Correct the comments about private windows and the capture chords. Reduce ShouldTrimDisplayUrlAfterHostName to the feature check. Fixes: F64, L1, L41, L42, L43, L44, L59, L60. Done when: `AcceleratorsCocoaBrowserTest.MappingAcceleratorsInMainMenu` passes, it is added to the release sweep's upstream suites, and tooling's capture still works without the constructor hook.
- [ ] **CMD-40** (P3) Make the Google-row filters match what M2 and M3 claim. Give ProfileSubMenuModel an IsCommandIdVisible that consults the same rule. Either hide send-to-self, compose and translate or reword M2, and treat QR code the same in both menus. Remove hidden rows at build time so no doubled separators remain. Fixes: L34, L49, L50, L57, L62. Done when: right-clicking an image on the Mac shows no adjacent separators. A signed-in profile's app-menu profile submenu shows no Google account rows. The page menu matches M2's list.
- [ ] **CMD-41** (P3) Small key and copy fixes. `return model->CycleRecent(delta)` in CycleRecentTab. The Markdown escaping is WIN-13's change and the split-pane rename refresh is TAB-4's; here, only check them. Have File → Copy Link copy an open peek's page. Drop the letter keys from the Windows Ctrl+Alt+Shift map on AltGr layouts, and correct the Z3 note. Document the Intel rotation-hotkey conflict and any extension shortcuts the new chords shadow. Fixes: L29, L36, L37, L39, L47, L48, L51. Done when: ⌃⇥ in a Space whose only row is a split moves between the panes. Copying "RFC [draft] notes" as Markdown renders as one link on GitHub. With a peek of example.org open, File → Copy Link copies example.org. Renaming one pane updates both rows. On a Polish layout, left Ctrl+Alt+Shift+C types "Ć".

#### Findings

| ID | Sev | State | Where (path:line, patch) | Problem | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CMD-F1 | critical | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:224, :579 (0022); chrome/browser/ui/browser_actions.cc:2004 | Actions mode lists every visible, enabled item in Chromium's action registry and invokes it with an empty context. "Ask" hits a fatal CHECK, and "Pin" crashes too. | Replace the registry walk with a Stedding allow-list of IDC commands run through `command()`. |
| CMD-F2 | critical | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_strip_model.cc:2742; chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_menu_model.cc:185-221 (0024, 0027) | In the horizontal strip, submenu ids between the two ends of the Move-to-Space and Route ranges reach TabStripModel's switch and hit a fatal NOTREACHED as soon as the menu builds. | Route the Stedding verbs through one helper that both strip controllers call, and range-guard the ids. |
| CMD-F3 | critical | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_view.cc:162, :202 (0009) | The card gets no flex, so the peeked page is laid out 0 px tall and only the 44 px header shows, for every user. | `SetFlexForView(card_, 1)`, plus a widget-level test that asserts the web view's height is above 0. |
| CMD-F4 | critical | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_view.cc:133, :237 (0009); chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_storage_partition.cc:150 | The peek always uses the shared jar, so links from an isolated Space's pinned tab load under the shared identity, and promotion rebinds the tab and reloads the page. | Create the peek's contents in the source tab's Space partition. |
| CMD-F5 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:174, :692 (0005) | Closing the bar without navigating leaves no view focused, so the page does not get the keyboard back. | Track the view that had focus before Show and restore it (or the page) in Close. |
| CMD-F6 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/browser_command_controller.cc:1249 (0022); chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:1106 (0005) | After ⌘L, Enter opens a new tab instead of navigating the current one. | Add a navigate-current-tab mode set only by IDC_FOCUS_LOCATION. |
| CMD-F7 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:122, :759 (0005, 0013) | Tab search matches titles only, folds only ASCII case, and shows the first four matches in strip order. OPEN_TAB matches are dropped. | Use one i18n matcher over title, host and URL, and score and sort before capping. |
| CMD-F8 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:659, :947 (0005, 0013) | The selection never scrolls into view, and every arrow press rebuilds every row, so Enter can run an invisible row. | Keep the row views, restyle only the changed rows, and scroll the chosen row into view. |
| CMD-F9 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:920, :948, :1171 (0013, 0028) | Painting, the arrow keys and Enter use three different row mappings (archived rows, the skipped duplicate), so Enter opens a different row from the highlighted one. | Build one Row vector and use it for painting, selection and Enter. |
| CMD-F10 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:782, :1029 (0005, 0013) | Results store a raw strip index. A strip change while the bar is open activates the wrong tab or hits a fatal NOTREACHED or CHECK. | Store a TabHandle and observe the strip while the bar is open. |
| CMD-F11 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:1030 (0005); chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:1514 | Choosing an essential switches the window to the Space where the essential was pinned. The @Space filter also filters essentials by that Space. | Switch Space only when the tab is not in the active Space, and let pinned tabs match any Space. |
| CMD-F12 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/browser_command_controller.cc:1249 (0022); chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:1105 | ⌘L, edit and Enter opens a new tab, and the prefill is the raw spec (same root as F6). | Add a navigate-current-tab mode, format the prefill like the location bar, and show a "Go" row. |
| CMD-F13 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/browser_command_controller.cc:2097-2101; chrome/browser/ui/stedding/little_window.cc:98-104 (0035) | The little-window commands are enabled before the window is marked little, so ⇧⌘O and Escape stay disabled for good. ⌃1–9 are disabled in popups. | Mark the window at creation, and give it its own view accelerators. |
| CMD-F14 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_commands.cc:85-113; chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:952-975 (0034, 0004) | Clear can empty the strip and close the window: the private guard runs IDC_NEW_TAB, which now opens the bar, and normal windows have no guard. | Insert a New Tab first when Clear would close every tab. |
| CMD-F15 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/browser_command_controller.cc:1242-1258 (0022); chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:1094-1109 (0005) | ⌘L / Ctrl+L opens the bar, and Enter opens the edited URL in a new tab, including from File > Open Location (same root as F6). | Add a kEditCurrent mode that navigates CURRENT_TAB. |
| CMD-F16 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/app_controller_mac.mm:1272-1277; chrome/browser/ui/stedding/little_window.cc:105 (0035) | Untrusted launches (the direct-launch scheme, Handoff) opened in a little window lose upstream's opaque initiator and load as if typed, with SameSite=Strict cookies attached. | Pass `is_untrusted_launch` and set an opaque `initiator_origin`, or decide inside the upstream startup path. |
| CMD-F17 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/app_controller_mac.mm:1272-1277; chrome/browser/ui/browser_window/internal/create_browser_window_non_android.cc:142 (0035) | The little-window path skips the safe-profile and creation-status checks, so forced incognito, shutdown or a profile being deleted hits CreateBrowserWindow's CHECK. | Resolve the profile as upstream does, and return early when the status is not kOk. |
| CMD-F18 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/stedding/little_window.cc:100-104; chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_commands.cc:175; chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:449 (0035) | Same init-order bug as F13. The "Open in <Space>" rows can never show either. | Mark the window before its features initialise, and enable or cut the Space-choice verbs. |
| CMD-F19 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/navigator/browser_navigator.cc:1153, :497 (0027); chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_storage_partition.cc:174-230 | Routing moves a tab after its contents exist. A typed address routed across jars is lost to the rebind, and a routed new tab's first request uses the wrong jar. | Decide the Space and create the contents in its jar before navigating, and do not rebind afterwards. |
| CMD-F20 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_navigation_throttle.cc:73, :116; chrome/browser/ui/browser_tabstrip.cc:112 (0009) | `target=_blank` and `window.open` arrive through AddWebContents with `is_renderer_initiated` false, so they never peek, and the adopt path is dead code. | Also accept `contents_to_insert` with a gesture, excluding NEW_POPUP, and adopt without reloading. |
| CMD-F21 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_view.cc:133, :351, :364 (0009); components/blocked_content/popup_blocker.cc:131 | The peek's WebContents has no tab helpers and a stub delegate, so the popup blocker is bypassed, and dialogs, file choosers and permission prompts do not work. | Host the peek in a real tab environment and forward the delegate calls to the browser. |
| CMD-F22 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_view.cc:205; chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_navigation_throttle.cc:169-182 (0009) | The peek re-sends the pinned page's cross-site navigation as a browser-initiated load with no initiator, referrer or gesture check, which bypasses SameSite=Strict and Fetch Metadata. | Require a user gesture and carry the original navigation's initiator, referrer and gesture into the peek. |
| CMD-F23 | high | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/peek/peek_view.cc:183-187, :415 (0009) | The header shows the page title before the host and elides from the end, so a long `<title>` hides the real origin. | Show the origin in its own label with priority and a security indicator. |
| CMD-F24 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:724, :159 (0022) | Escape out of actions mode, or ⌘T / ⌘L on an open bar, shows stale tab rows under the current text, because the Escape accelerator path skips UpdateResults. | Refresh the results in SetMode(kTabs), and delete the dead Escape branch. |
| CMD-F25 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:378, :328, :355 (0032, 0026, 0033) | Turn Bookmarks into Pinned Tabs, Import from Arc and Back Up run on one Enter with no confirmation and no result. | Add a confirmation step and a result toast, and hide the conversion row once done. |
| CMD-F26 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:134, :644, :960 (0005) | The bar exposes no dialog or list semantics and no selected row to assistive technology, and rows give no hover feedback. | Add listbox roles, the active descendant, and hover styling that updates the selection. |
| CMD-F27 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:450-468 (0035); chrome/browser/ui/stedding/little_window.cc:87 | The little window's "Open in <Space>" rows are dead code, because the bar never opens in a popup window. | Delete the block and set little.md E1 accordingly (or make the bar open in little windows). |
| CMD-F28 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_commands.cc:105 (0034); chrome/browser/ui/browser_command_controller.cc:798 (0005) | Clearing a private window whose tabs are all unpinned closes it, because the keep-alive runs IDC_NEW_TAB, which opens the bar (reached through the Clear line). | Create the keep-alive tab directly, not through IDC_NEW_TAB. |
| CMD-F29 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:495, :122 (0022, 0005); docs/features/*.md | Spec rows marked built (pins H6, tabs R5 and R14, commandbar K9 and K10) claim bar rows and fuzzy matching that do not exist. | Add the rows or mark the spec rows partial. |
| CMD-F30 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:723, :159, :1151 (0022) | Escape out of actions mode, or ⌘T on an open bar, redraws tab rows from an older query (same root as F24). | Call UpdateResults when leaving actions mode. |
| CMD-F31 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:755, :896, :1174 (0048) | "@Space query" filters the rows, but Enter's default row web-searches the literal "@Space query". | With a Space prefix, select the first matching tab and classify only the rest of the query. |
| CMD-F32 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:759, :779, :437 (0048) | Hide Other Spaces does not hide other Spaces' tabs, names or Move rows in the bar that toggles it. | Filter the tabs, archived rows and Move rows to the active Space while the coat is on. |
| CMD-F33 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:947, :659, :918 (0013) | The list never scrolls to the keyboard selection (same root as F8). | Scroll the chosen row into view and keep the row views. |
| CMD-F34 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:1030; chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:610 (0005, 0004) | Switching to a tab in another Space first activates that Space's remembered tab, which reloads it if it is asleep. Collapsed folders stay collapsed. | Add an "activate tab in its Space" entry point on SpaceModel. |
| CMD-F35 | medium | confirmed | chrome/browser/ui/views/commandbar/command_bar_view.cc:759, :833, :1019 (0005, 0013) | Tabs in other windows cannot be found, and their history matches open duplicates. | Search every normal window of the profile, and turn `has_tab_match` into Switch to Tab. |

### Settings, welcome, archive and import

Nothing in this area explains the tab-click, rail or crash symptoms the owner reported. Every review pass looked, and none of these units contains tab-selection, rail, paint or Space-animation code. The area is still in poor shape. It is the product's front door (settings, the first-run welcome, import) and its safety nets (auto-archive, restore, backups), and most of it is wired end to end but cannot be trusted. Four problems come before everything else. First, the keychain rename in patch 0038 creates a brand-new encryption key when the user clicks Deny on the macOS prompt, and that loses every saved password and cookie (WEB-F2). Second, the settings Spaces handler has two use-after-free paths (WEB-F1, WEB-F3). Third, three privacy promises are false. On macOS the browser still installs extensions that vendors register for Google Chrome (WEB-F5). DuckDuckGo is not the fallback engine in eleven regions (WEB-F9). Safe Browsing probably does nothing in keyless builds (WEB-F18). Fourth, auto-archive measures "idle" wrongly in three ways and can raise a "Leave site?" dialog over a hidden Space (WEB-F10, WEB-F11, WEB-F12). Its restores lose the tab's Space, folder and cookie jar (WEB-F13, WEB-F19). Around those: the README says a user can import from Chrome, Brave or Edge on macOS, and they cannot (WEB-F6). Chromium's own importer can no longer be reached because it was hidden along with the People page (WEB-F4). One bookmark import turns every bookmark into a tab in a single UI task and hides the bookmark bar for good (WEB-F8, WEB-F17, WEB-F51). Sidebar backups keep about a day of history although the schedule promises a year, and restoring one duplicates the open tabs (WEB-F15, WEB-F16). The tests in this area test helpers, fakes and fabricated data. Several assert the bugs themselves (WEB-F35, WEB-F40, WEB-F50, WEB-F67), so many spec rows marked "built" are not. What is solid: the pref rows on the Stedding page bind registered, allowlisted prefs; the welcome dialog's lifetime and WebUI plumbing hold up; the archived page renders with textContent only; the Arc parser handles the documented shape, and its v10 decryption is correct. Nothing was executed during the audit. "Confirmed" means traced in code, not reproduced.

#### Features

| Feature | Real state | Why (finding ids) | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stedding section pref rows (settings T1–T8, T10–T12) | Mostly works: every row binds a registered, allowlisted pref. chrome://settings opens on Autofill, not on Stedding. The width slider rewrites a dragged width to the nearest tick. Two rows control one hover behaviour. The quiet-prompt rows break an upstream invariant | WEB-F20, WEB-F22, WEB-F23, WEB-F36, WEB-L4–L6, WEB-L8–L10 | fix |
| Spaces list in settings: rename, delete, Independent session (T9, sessions S10) | Partly: rename works. The handler crashes after the settings tab moves windows. Delete does nothing for the active Space and needs no confirmation for the others. The isolation switch lies in a Blank Window and reloads the settings tab into the Space's cookie jar | WEB-F1, WEB-F3, WEB-F24–F27, WEB-F68 | fix: rewrite the handler's lifetime and replies |
| Routes editor in settings (routing D5) | Partly: rules can be added. Removal goes by a stale index, and the rules of deleted Spaces cannot be seen or removed | WEB-F31, WEB-L37 | fix |
| Hidden Google and AI sections | Partly: hidden, but hiding People also removed the importData and manageProfile routes. The Autofill landing page still shows Chromium's sign-in card | WEB-F4, WEB-F21 | fix |
| Chromium's "Import Bookmarks and Settings" (import I13, menus M3) | Broken: every entry point lands on Autofill with no dialog | WEB-F4 | fix |
| Shortcut reference (shortcuts Z1–Z6) | Partly: the chords come from the tables. Descriptions and the Chromium column are typed by hand, removed chords have no row, and ⌘9 is described wrongly | WEB-F34, WEB-L30, WEB-L31 | simplify: correct the copy, add the missing rows, read the two prefs |
| About line and update status (T10) | Partly: the version line is right, but nothing tells a Mac user where updates come from | WEB-F33 | fix |
| Little-window toggle on Windows | Dead control: nothing on Windows reads it | WEB-F32 | simplify: hide the row off the Mac |
| Welcome flow (W1, W4–W9) | Partly: the page and dialog are sound. Skip sends the user into Chromium's unskippable chooser. The default-browser step never updates after the user confirms. Windows shows Mac copy. Every click rebuilds the DOM and loses focus. It has never been tested in a real first run | WEB-F7, WEB-F37, WEB-F39, WEB-F40, WEB-F42 | fix |
| Welcome search step and DuckDuckGo default (W2, PRIVACY.md) | Partly: every engine shows the same globe icon. DuckDuckGo is not guaranteed in CN, HK, KH, KR, LA, MN, MO, TW, UZ, VN or for an unknown country. The upstream tests contradict what ships and are never run | WEB-F7, WEB-F9, WEB-F41, WEB-F43 | fix |
| Welcome import step (W3) | Broken for the main audience: on macOS it lists Safari, Firefox and a fake "Bookmarks HTML File" tile, and never Chrome, Brave or Edge | WEB-F6, WEB-F38, WEB-F52 | finish: a Stedding-owned Chromium-family importer; correct the docs now |
| Privacy defaults (Q5, Q9, Safe Browsing) | Partly: the quiet prompts leave CPSS on too. Q9 was fixed on Windows only. Safe Browsing is probably inert without an API key | WEB-F5, WEB-F18, WEB-F23 | fix |
| Auto-archive (A1–A6) | Partly: the sweep closes tabs, but it measures idle from activation, loses the count on every launch and while the Mac sleeps, and does not protect visible, playing or in-call tabs. beforeunload can raise the window | WEB-F10, WEB-F11, WEB-F12, WEB-F44 | fix: rewrite the idle bookkeeping |
| Archived view and restore (A7–A11) | Partly: listing and search work. Restore matches by display name, so it can pick the wrong or a duplicate Space and put isolated tabs on the shared jar. Clear wipes all of recently-closed. Retention only hides entries | WEB-F13, WEB-F19, WEB-F46–F50 | fix |
| Little-window idle close (little E3) and the popup archiver | Dead: a one-tab popup's tab is always active, so the window never closes. Every popup still runs a 5-minute timer | WEB-F45, WEB-F70 | cut: delete the little-window branch in `TabArchiver`, create no archiver for popups, withdraw E3 (redesign per window later) |
| Import from Arc: sidebar (I1–I4, I8–I12) | Partly: works on the documented shape. Split views are dropped with their tabs, a self-referencing list expands exponentially, the ⌘T row imports everything (keychain prompt included) with no summary, and re-import misses folder contents | WEB-F53–F55, WEB-F59–F63 | fix |
| Import from Arc: history and passwords (I6, I21–I23) | Partly: the common case works. It is skipped when the sidebar file is missing, every re-run duplicates visits, and the counts are inflated | WEB-F56–F58 | fix |
| Keychain item under Stedding's name (I24) | Broken: Deny creates a new key and loses all encrypted data. A fresh install adopts Chromium's key | WEB-F2, WEB-F14 | fix before any release |
| Bookmarks to pins (I13–I16) | Partly: converts the whole tree with no cap, synchronously, even after an empty import, and hides the bar and the star permanently | WEB-F8, WEB-F17, WEB-F38, WEB-F51, WEB-F52 | simplify: no automatic conversion after an import; keep the ⌘T command with a count, a cap and a way back |
| Scheduled sidebar backups and restore (I17–I19) | Broken: rolling buckets keep about a day. There is one writer per window with non-atomic writes. Restore duplicates open tabs after a restart. "Back up now" reports success before writing. The files survive Clear Browsing Data | WEB-F15, WEB-F16, WEB-F28, WEB-F29, WEB-F65, WEB-F66 | cut: stop the scheduler; remove "Back up now", the backup list and restore from settings and the command bar; leave existing files alone; withdraw I17–I19. A later profile-level design can bring it back |
| Export Space and Import sidebar file (I20) | Partly: works for plain names. A "/" in the name fails, the download folder is ignored, the isolation flag is dropped, import dedupes on per-run ids, and the file dialog is never detached | WEB-F3, WEB-F16, WEB-F30, WEB-F64 | fix |
| `stedding.sidebar` v2 file module (Sf1, Sf5) | Model only: no production caller | WEB-L111, WEB-L112 | cut: delete `sidebar_file.*` and its tests, withdraw Sf1 and Sf5 |

#### Work, in order

- [ ] **WEB-1** (P0) Make the keychain lookup fail closed and copy a legacy key forward once. Fixes: WEB-F2, WEB-F14. The same function is changed by the platform section's PLT-2 (the marker gate for migrated profiles); land the two changes together. In `components/os_crypt/common/keychain_password_mac.mm`, mint a new key only when both lookups return `errSecItemNotFound`. For any other error from the legacy lookup, return an empty string (upstream's "temporarily unavailable"). When the legacy item is found, write it into "Stedding Safe Storage". Extend `FakeKeychainV2` so it answers per service name. Done when: on a Mac with a profile sealed under "Chromium Safe Storage", clicking Deny at the macOS prompt leaves Keychain Access with no new "Stedding Safe Storage" item, and after a relaunch where the user clicks Allow, saved passwords are listed and a signed-in site is still signed in; after Allow once, deleting the legacy item and relaunching keeps both.
- [ ] **WEB-2** (P0) Rewrite `SteddingSpacesHandler`'s lifetime and input handling. Fixes: WEB-F1, WEB-F3, WEB-F68, WEB-F72, WEB-L32, WEB-L33, WEB-L34, WEB-L35. Subscribe to the settings tab's `TabInterface` `RegisterWillDetach` (reset the observation) and `RegisterDidInsert` (observe the new window's model and push). Add `SpaceModel::Observer::OnSpaceModelDestroying()`. Call `select_file_dialog_->ListenerDestroyed()` in the destructor and in `OnJavascriptDisallowed`, and invalidate weak pointers there. Type-check every argument. Take backup names, not paths, and resolve them under `BackupDirectory()`. Done when: an interactive_ui_test (ASan) opens chrome://settings/stedding in window A, moves the tab to window B, closes A, then reloads and closes the settings tab with no crash, and the list shows B's Spaces after the move; a second test closes the settings tab while the "Import sidebar…" picker is open, then cancels the picker, with no crash; a test that sends `steddingRestoreBackup` with an absolute path outside the backup folder reads nothing.
- [ ] **WEB-3** (P0) Stop reading Google Chrome's machine-wide External Extensions folder on macOS. Fixes: WEB-F5. In `chrome/browser/extensions/external_provider_impl.cc`, extend the Stedding hunk so the `DIR_EXTERNAL_EXTENSIONS` pref loader is also dropped on the Mac. Keep the per-user provider. Widen privacy Q9 to macOS. Done when: on a Mac where `/Library/Application Support/Google/Chrome/External Extensions/<id>.json` exists (create one for a test extension), a fresh Stedding profile makes no request to the update URL and shows no "Action required" extension prompt; a browser_test asserts that no provider points at a Chrome-owned location. **Status 2026-09-28:** the provider hunk drops Chrome's folder on the Mac (privacy Q9 names both platforms), and the browser_test half passes: `ExternalExtensionsTest.ChromesFolderIsNotRead` points Chrome's machine-wide folder and Stedding's own per-user folder at temporary ones, one extension each, and the providers a profile gets report Stedding's and not Chrome's (with the Mac hunk taken out, they report Chrome's too). Open: the check with a file in the real `/Library/Application Support/Google/Chrome/External Extensions`, which needs an administrator's write to /Library.
- [x] **WEB-4** (P0) Make DuckDuckGo the fallback engine in every region, and bring the upstream tests for these hunks back to green. Fixes: WEB-F9, WEB-F43, WEB-L68. In `GetPrepopulatedFallbackSearch`, call `FindPrepopulatedEngineInternal` with `use_first_as_fallback=false` and return the built-in DuckDuckGo entry when that yields nothing. Move the worldwide-Waffle change into one Stedding-owned predicate. Rewrite `FindGoogleAsFallback` and the regional-capabilities cases to assert Stedding's behaviour. Add a `components_unittests` leg to `tooling/dev`, and move `SearchTest.*` to browser_tests or drop it. Rewrite `docs/PRIVACY.md` line 70 to match. Done when: launching fresh profiles with `--search-engine-choice-country=KR`, `=CN` and `=ZZ`, skipping the welcome's search step and typing a query in the omnibox goes to duckduckgo.com in each; the new `components_unittests` leg passes with the rewritten cases and a new test over every `regional_settings.json` entry.
- [ ] **WEB-5** (P0) Find out whether Safe Browsing works in a keyless build, then either make it work or say it is off. Fixes: WEB-F18. Confirm first: on a fresh profile of a shipped build, open chrome://safe-browsing, check the database update status, capture the requests to safebrowsing.googleapis.com (a 400 or 403 "API key not valid" confirms it), and load testsafebrowsing.appspot.com's phishing page. If it is confirmed, choose one: ship a key through gn args, proxy Safe Browsing, or state in Settings, `docs/PRIVACY.md` and `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` that it is off. Correct the W9 rationale and the `infobar_utils.cc` comment either way. Done when: the test phishing page shows an interstitial in a release build, or Settings → Privacy says Safe Browsing is unavailable and PRIVACY.md no longer claims it ships on.
- [x] **WEB-6** (P0) Make the archive sweep skip every tab the user is using, and make it never activate anything. Fixes: WEB-F12, WEB-F44, WEB-L76, WEB-L84, WEB-L85. In `TabArchiver::IsProtected`, return true for `NeedToFireBeforeUnloadOrUnloadEvents()`, `IsVisible()`, the peer of an active split pane, audible or recently audible contents, picture-in-picture, and tabs that are being captured or are capturing. Make the archive mark single-use: consume it in `PopulateArchiveExtraData`, and clear it on any tab still in the strip after the close call returns. Done when: with the idle setting at a test value, an interactive_ui_test leaves (a) a page with a half-filled form and a beforeunload handler in an inactive Space, (b) the right pane of a split while the left is active, and (c) a tab playing audio; after the sweep all three are open, no dialog appeared, the window was not activated and the active Space did not change; a plain ⌘W on a tab the sweep had skipped is recorded as a plain close.
- [x] **WEB-7** (P0) Write the tab's Space id, pin, home, split and folder path into the recently-closed entry, and restore by id with the cookie jar settled before the tab is activated. Fixes: WEB-F13, WEB-F19, WEB-L77, WEB-L79, WEB-L80, WEB-L81. Confirm first, for WEB-F19: in an independent-session Space, sign in to a local test server that logs Cookie headers, close the tab, switch Space, and use "Restore to Work" from chrome://stedding-archive while net-export records whether any request carries shared-jar cookies. In `PopulateArchiveExtraData`, copy `SessionExtraDataForTab` and the folder path, and store the raw name only as a fallback for deleted Spaces. Use a const Space lookup there. In `RestoreArchivedTab`, resolve the Space without creating it, restore, create the Space only when the tab came back, rebind the jar synchronously if the partition differs, and then activate. Done when: in the running browser, closing a tab that sits in a folder in an isolated, unnamed Space, then reordering the Spaces and restoring it from chrome://stedding-archive and from ⌘T, puts it back in that Space and folder, still signed in to the Space's site; the local test server sees no shared-jar cookie; a browser_test with a real `TabRestoreService` covers the same path. **Proven 2026-09-28, patch 0057:** `ArchiveRestoreTest.TheArchivePageRestoresIt` (chrome://stedding-archive, its row's Restore button) and `.TheCommandBarRestoresIt` (⌘T, the archived row, Enter) after the Spaces were reordered; `RestoreTest.AnArchivedTabComesBackToItsSpaceFolderAndJar` (a real TabRestoreService). The server sees `who=work` and not the shared jar's cookie.
- [ ] **WEB-8** (P1) Rewrite the archiver's idle clock: wall-clock time since the tab was last shown, persisted through restore. Fixes: WEB-F10, WEB-F11, WEB-L82, WEB-L83. Store `base::Time` from an injectable `base::Clock`. Touch `selection.old_tab` on activation change if it is still in the strip. Re-stamp protected tabs on every sweep. Seed restored tabs from `WebContents::GetLastActiveTime()`. Use `continue`, not `break`, for a dead handle. Rewrite `NeverArchivesTheActiveTab` so it expects the tab just left to survive. Done when: an interactive_ui_test with a test clock keeps tab A active for 13 h and then switches to tab B, and A survives the next sweep; a test that restarts the browser with a restored tab last seen 13 h ago (clock advanced across the restart) archives it at the first sweep; on a Mac with the 6 h setting, a tab left for a night with the lid closed is archived the next morning.

Have `app.ts` send the visible ids, and have `HandleClearArchive` call `RemoveEntryById` for those only, behind a confirmation. Prune TAB entries older than `ArchiveRetention()` on load and on each sweep, or relabel the row "Show closed tabs from the last…". Done when: in the running browser, after closing a window of 5 tabs and one single tab, filtering chrome://stedding-archive to one Space and clicking Clear asks first, removes only the listed rows, and History → Recently Closed still reopens the window with ⇧⌘T; with a 1-day retention, an entry from two days ago is gone from ⇧⌘T after the next sweep (or the label says "Show").

Count what the backend accepted, blocked sites separately, and undecryptable rows as skipped. Reject non-UTF-8 plaintext. Tell `errSecItemNotFound` apart from denial. Done when: in the running browser, with Arc's sidebar file renamed, the welcome still offers History and Passwords and imports them; running the import twice leaves each Arc visit once in the history database; the result line's history count matches what chrome://history shows for the imported range.
- [ ] **WEB-28** (P2) Make the welcome and the toasts use the platform's own words, and open the shortcuts page where the user can see it. Fixes: WEB-F37, WEB-L1, WEB-L2, WEB-L54, WEB-L56, WEB-L58. Send the platform sentences through loadTimeData with IS_MAC and IS_WIN branches. Hide the Arc block off the Mac. Give Linux its own chords. Add a "Show in folder" toast string for Windows. Match print preview's key check to its label. Finish the flow before opening chrome://settings/stedding. Done when: the Windows preview's welcome contains no "Mac" or "macOS" and no Arc block, and its screenshot toast says "Show in folder"; clicking "Every shortcut, in Settings" on either platform closes the welcome and shows the reference in front.
- [ ] **WEB-29** (P2) Update the welcome page in place instead of rebuilding it, and fix the tile semantics. Fixes: WEB-F39, WEB-L41. Build each step once; toggle classes and `aria-checked`; move focus to the step heading on a step change; give the radiogroups arrow keys and a roving tabindex; name the dialog. Done when: in the running browser, a keyboard-only user goes through all five steps with Tab, arrows and Enter without focus ever falling to the page body; VoiceOver announces the selected tile as checked.
- [ ] **WEB-30** (P2) Refresh the default-browser step after the system prompt. Fixes: WEB-F42. Re-send `getDefaultBrowserState` on window focus and on `visibilitychange`, show a pending state after the click, and map UNKNOWN and OTHER_MODE_IS_DEFAULT separately. Done when: on a Mac, clicking "Set as default" and confirming macOS's "Use Stedding" turns the step to "Stedding is your default browser" without another click.
- [ ] **WEB-31** (P2) Make the archived page re-render only the list, keep focus and validate its messages. Fixes: WEB-F48, WEB-L70, WEB-L71, WEB-L72, WEB-L73, WEB-L88, WEB-L89, WEB-L90. Build the header once, diff rows by id, focus search only on the first load, and move focus to the next row after Forget. Reset the Space filter when its Space is gone (filter by id after WEB-7). Label buttons with the tab title, and name the rail's Archived row. Check argument shapes, and accept only ids that the list returns. Done when: in the running browser, with chrome://stedding-archive scrolled halfway, closing a tab in another window changes neither the scroll position nor focus; after Forget, focus sits on the next row's Restore button; VoiceOver reads each tab's title on its buttons.
- [ ] **WEB-32** (P2) Cut the little-window idle close and the popup archiver. Fixes: WEB-F45, WEB-F70. Delete the little-window branch in `TabArchiver` (the exemption, `kLittleIdleHours`'s use) and create no archiver for `TYPE_POPUP` windows. Delete `LittleWindowUsesItsOwnThreshold`. Mark little E3 "not implemented". Done when: opening an OAuth popup and a little window and leaving them for the archive threshold closes neither; a trace of the idle browser shows no 5-minute archive timer for popups.
- [ ] **WEB-33** (P2) Hide the little-window toggle where little windows do not exist. Fixes: WEB-F32, WEB-L7. Add `steddingHasLittleWindows` (true only on the Mac) and render the row only when it is set. Done when: chrome://settings/stedding on the Windows preview has no "Open links from other apps in a small window" row, and the Mac still has it.
- [ ] **WEB-34** (P2) Tell the user where updates come from. Fixes: WEB-F33, WEB-L19, WEB-L27. Until ADR 0014's updater lands, add an About row "Updates: download the latest from GitHub Releases" with the link, in Stedding-owned code, and correct the updater comments. Done when: chrome://settings/help on a Mac build shows the row, and its link opens the releases page.
- [ ] **WEB-35** (P2) Make the shortcut reference true: read the two prefs, add rows for the removed chords, fix ⌘9, and reword "cannot drift". Fixes: WEB-F34, WEB-L30, WEB-L31. Done when: in the running browser, turning off "Ctrl-Tab in recent order" changes the Ctrl+Tab row's description on the reference; the reference lists ⇧⌘L and the Mac tab-group chords as removed; the numbers row matches what ⌘9 does.
- [ ] **WEB-36** (P2) Add real tests for the settings page and the Spaces handler. Fixes: WEB-F35. Cover, through the page: rename; delete of the active and of another Space; a route added outside the page, then removed; the isolation switch with and without a registry; the tab move from WEB-2; chrome://settings/importData; the landing route; the slider leaving a non-tick width alone. Enumerate the accelerator tables for every `IDC_STEDDING_*` command in the shortcut test instead of a fixed list. Done when: these tests exist as browser_tests or settings WebUI tests, fail on the current HEAD for the delete, route, slider and import cases, and pass after WEB-2, WEB-12, WEB-20, WEB-22 and WEB-24.
- [ ] **WEB-37** (P2) Test the welcome in a real first run. Fixes: WEB-F40, WEB-L39. Add a `tooling/drive` mode without `--no-first-run`. Add handler tests for `setSearchEngine` with and without the choice service, for `finish`, and a browser test for `MaybeShowWelcomeDialog` (normal, OTR, popup, `--no-first-run`, already shown, forced). Correct the test columns of W1 and W2. Done when: a fresh-profile run without `--no-first-run` is captured once, showing exactly one onboarding surface, and the new tests fail when `kWelcomeShown` is not set on Finish.
- [ ] **WEB-38** (P2) Test the archive through the real `TabRestoreService`. Fixes: WEB-F50. Close a tab in a named, non-active Space inside a folder by sweep, by Clear and by ⌘W, and assert the entry's extra data. Rename the Space, restore through `RestoreArchivedTab` and the WebUI handler, and assert the Space, folder, partition and active tab. Assert the timer's `IsRunning` for A5. Remove the production observer hook. Done when: these browser_tests fail on the current HEAD for the Space id and folder cases and pass after WEB-7.
- [ ] **WEB-39** (P2) Test the import paths users actually hit. Fixes: WEB-F67. Replace every WebContents before a restore; iterate pruning over a simulated clock (or delete it with WEB-18); make the fake keychain answer per service (found, not found, denied); assert `ShouldShowBookmarkBar`; run `ImportArcDataAsync` against a `TestingProfile` with history and password stores. Done when: the keychain denied-case test fails on the current HEAD and passes after WEB-1; the history test fails on a second run before WEB-27 and passes after it.
- [ ] **WEB-40** (P3) Move the Stedding settings strings into `stedding_strings.grdp` and delete the dead flags and comments. Fixes: WEB-F69, WEB-L13, WEB-L20, WEB-L21, WEB-L22, WEB-L23, WEB-L24, WEB-L25, WEB-L29, WEB-L38. Add a Stedding-owned `AddSettingsStrings()` so `settings_ui.cc` keeps a one-line call. Delete `steddingHasShortcutReference`, `steddingHidesGoogleSections` and the unused strings. Move the peek accelerators out of the views target. Return page visibility from a Stedding helper that spreads upstream's defaults. Route chrome://version through `AboutVersionString`, or narrow T10. Done when: a pseudo-locale build (`--lang=en-XA`) shows every Stedding settings and shortcut string pseudo-localised; chrome://settings shows the same menu as before.
- [ ] **WEB-41** (P3) Make the settings privacy block's rows match their controls. Fixes: WEB-L8, WEB-L9, WEB-L10, WEB-L18, WEB-L28. Drop or relabel the cookie "Allow" option. Bind HTTPS-First to the generated pref. Make sub-labels state-independent and label the machine-wide rows. Show when macOS Reduce Motion overrides the Animate row. Done when: in the running browser, setting HTTPS-First to Strict on Chromium's page shows the Stedding row on; with macOS Reduce Motion on, the Animate row says the system setting is in force.
- [ ] **WEB-42** (P3) Clean up the welcome and search hunks, and refuse the welcome page outside its dialog. Fixes: WEB-L45, WEB-L46, WEB-L52, WEB-L53, WEB-L55, WEB-L57, WEB-L66, WEB-L67. Replace the search.cc dead branch with a direct return, delete stale comments and constants, and fix gn formatting. Override `IsWebUIEnabled` to refuse OTR and guest profiles. Show "open from the first window" when no `WelcomeHost` is attached. Tint the first Space by id and preselect its current swatch. Done when: typing chrome://stedding-welcome in a private window shows an error page; in the dialog, the swatch step preselects the first Space's current colour.
- [ ] **WEB-43** (P3) Correct the GPC and feature-flag documentation and move the flag flips into one Stedding-owned list. Fixes: WEB-L40, WEB-L47, WEB-L48, WEB-L49, WEB-L69. Rewrite Q4 and `docs/PRIVACY.md` to say that local state becomes a process-wide feature at startup and that a change needs a restart. Reference the feature by name. Re-derive the Q10 set against M155's testing config. Done when: a browser_test asserts that a navigation carries `Sec-GPC: 1` with the pref on; chrome://version's command line shows the Stedding feature list in one place.
- [ ] **WEB-44** (P3) Bring the archive docs and code comments in line with the code, and delete the archive's dead helpers and test hooks. Fixes: WEB-F71, WEB-L74, WEB-L75, WEB-L86, WEB-L87, WEB-L91, WEB-L92. Downgrade A1, A2 and A8–A10 until WEB-6 to WEB-8 land. Remove "archive" from tabs R21. Fix the four comments. Add the missing gn deps. Delete `ArchiveReasonFromName`, `last_active_for_testing` and the global observer hook. Done when: `tooling/check-repo` passes, and every "built" row in `docs/features/archive.md` names a browser_test from WEB-38 that passes.
- [ ] **WEB-45** (P3) Tidy the Arc importer: upstream crypto APIs, profiles, names, the essentials cap and the Arc-running warning. Fixes: WEB-L96, WEB-L97, WEB-L98, WEB-L99, WEB-L100, WEB-L101, WEB-L102, WEB-L103, WEB-L104, WEB-L105. Use `crypto::kdf::Pbkdf2HmacSha1` and `KeychainV2::FindGenericPassword`. Use `CONTINUE_ON_SHUTDOWN` for the read. Enumerate Arc's profiles, or name the skipped ones. Gate the feature with IS_MAC. Apply Arc's own tab title as the tab name. Insert extra essentials as Space pins and report them. Show the Arc-running warning on every entry point. Delete the dead branch. Done when: in the running browser, importing an Arc sidebar with a renamed pin shows the Arc name; with 10 essentials open, importing 14 Arc favourites reports where every one went; quitting during an import quits at once.
- [ ] **WEB-46** (P3) Delete the unused `stedding.sidebar` v2 module. Fixes: WEB-L111, WEB-L112. Remove `chrome/browser/ui/spaces/sidebar_file.*` and its tests, and mark Sf1 and Sf5 withdrawn (S-61). Done when: the browser builds without the files, and `docs/features/import.md` shows no `model only` rows for Sf1 or Sf5.

| ID | Sev | State | Where (path:line, patch) | Problem | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WEB-F1 | critical | confirmed | `chrome/browser/ui/webui/settings/stedding_spaces_handler.cc:107`, `:131`; `chrome/browser/ui/spaces/space_model.cc:197`; patch 0012 | The handler observes the SpaceModel of the window the settings tab was in when JavaScript was allowed. After a tab move and that window's close, `Reset()` calls `RemoveObserver` on a freed model. | Re-bind on the tab's detach and insert callbacks, and add an `OnSpaceModelDestroying()` notification (WEB-2). |
| WEB-F2 | critical | confirmed | `components/os_crypt/common/keychain_password_mac.mm:113`, `:119`, `:129`; patch 0038 | If the legacy "Chromium Safe Storage" read is denied or fails, a new key is created under the Stedding name, and existing passwords and cookies can never be decrypted again. | Mint only when both lookups return not-found, return empty on other errors, and copy a found legacy key forward (WEB-1). |
| WEB-F3 | high | confirmed | `chrome/browser/ui/webui/settings/stedding_spaces_handler.cc:50`, `:319`; `ui/shell_dialogs/select_file_dialog_win.cc:264`; patch 0033 | The "Import sidebar…" file dialog is never detached. On Windows it calls the freed handler when the user finishes; on the Mac the handler's destructor re-enters a member being destroyed. | Call `ListenerDestroyed()` in the destructor and on disallow (WEB-2). |
| WEB-F4 | high | confirmed | `chrome/browser/resources/settings/route.ts:174`; `chrome/browser/resources/settings/page_visibility.ts:75`; `chrome/browser/resources/settings/router.ts:455`; patch 0003 | Hiding People also deletes the importData and manageProfile routes, so every Import Bookmarks and Settings entry point and Customize profile land on Autofill with no dialog. | Create both routes under the Stedding route and host the import dialog there (WEB-12). |

On macOS the browser still reads `/Library/Application Support/Google/Chrome/External Extensions` and fetches vendor-registered extensions. | Drop the machine-wide pref loader on the Mac too, and widen Q9 (WEB-3). |

| WEB-F9 | high | confirmed | `components/search_engines/template_url_prepopulate_data.cc:316-328`; `components/regional_capabilities/program_settings.cc:114-118`; patch 0003 | The fallback searches only the shuffled regional list, which lacks DuckDuckGo in 10 regions and for unknown countries, so the default there is a random per-profile engine, Google included. | Fall back to the built-in DuckDuckGo entry, and correct `docs/PRIVACY.md` (WEB-4). |

The unit test asserts this. | Stamp the tab being left and every protected tab at each sweep (WEB-8). |
| WEB-F11 | high | confirmed | `chrome/browser/ui/archive/tab_archiver.cc:27-29`, `:95-98`, `:141-143`; patch 0011 | The idle clock is in-memory `TimeTicks`: every launch resets every tab, and macOS sleep does not count, so the 12 h, 1 day and 3 day settings rarely fire. | Use persisted wall-clock time seeded from `GetLastActiveTime()` (WEB-8). |
| WEB-F12 | high | confirmed | `chrome/browser/ui/archive/tab_archiver.cc:78-80`, `:117`; `chrome/browser/ui/archive/archive_mark.cc:61-73`; patches 0011, 0028 | The background sweep runs beforeunload. An app-modal "Leave site?" raises the window and activates a tab from a hidden Space. If the user cancels, a stale archive mark stays on the tab. | Skip tabs that need beforeunload, and make the mark single-use (WEB-6). |
| WEB-F13 | high | confirmed | `chrome/browser/ui/archive/archived_tabs.cc:41-56`, `:148-168`; `chrome/browser/ui/browser_live_tab_context.cc:177-179`; patch 0028 | Recently-closed entries carry only the reason and the positional display name, so restore matches by name: after a rename or reorder it creates a duplicate Space or lands in the wrong one. | Write the Space id, pin, home, split and folder path into the entry, and restore by id (WEB-7). |
